            or type(runner.get("actionId")) is not str or type(runner.get("candidateHash")) is not str
            or type(runner.get("timeoutSeconds")) is not int):
        missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
        return False
    return extra == [] and extra_spec == []


def _action_flags(
    root,
    action,
    program,
    sprints,
    campaigns,
    program_ok,
    sprints_ok,
    campaign_ok,
    missing,
):
    entry_eligible = False
    if program_ok and sprints_ok:
        entry_eligible = _entry_eligible(
            sprints,
            program,
            campaigns if campaign_ok else None,
            root,
            action["requirement"],
            missing,
        )
    command_ok = _resolve_command(root, action, missing)
    admitted, candidate_current, resource_admitted = _resolve_admission(
        root,
        action,
        program if program_ok else None,
        sprints if sprints_ok else None,
        campaigns if campaign_ok else None,
        missing,
    )
    authority = bool(
        program_ok
        and sprints_ok
        and campaign_ok
        and command_ok
        and admitted
        and not _structural_block(missing)
    )
    return {
        "authorityCurrent": authority,
        "entryEligible": entry_eligible,
        "candidateCurrent": candidate_current,
        "resourceAdmitted": resource_admitted,
    }


def _resolve_admission(root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing):
    ref = action["admissionRef"]
    if ref.startswith("campaign:"):
        return _resolve_campaign_admission(
            root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing
        )
    return _resolve_file_admission(root, ref, missing)


def _resolve_campaign_admission(root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing):
    ref = action["admissionRef"]
    campaign_id = ref[len("campaign:") :]
    if campaign_id == "":
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:campaign:")
        return False, False, False
    if campaigns is None or program is None or sprints is None:
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
        return False, False, False
    table = campaigns.get("campaigns")
    if type(table) is not dict or campaign_id not in table:
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
        return False, False, False
    record = table[campaign_id]
    if type(record) is not dict:
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
        return False, False, False
    gate = _find_gate(sprints, action["requirement"])
    stages = _program_stages(program)
    matched = _campaign_matches_gate(
        record, gate, action, stages, campaign_id, missing
    )
    binding_ok, binding_obj = _verify_binding(
        root, campaign_id, record, missing, expected_stage=gate.get("stage") if gate else None
    )
    candidate_ok = _verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, record, missing)
    resource_ok = _verify_resource(
        root, campaign_id, record, program, binding_obj, action, missing
    )
    return matched and binding_ok, candidate_ok, resource_ok


def _campaign_matches_gate(record, gate, action, stages, campaign_id, missing):
    if gate is None:
        missing.append("entryGate-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
        return False
    gate_stage = gate.get("stage")
    if type(gate_stage) is not str or gate_stage == "":
        missing.append("entryGate-stage-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
        return False
    rec_stage = record.get("stage")
    if type(rec_stage) is not str or rec_stage != gate_stage:
        missing.append("campaign-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    owners = gate.get("campaignOwner")
    if type(owners) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in owners):
        missing.append("entryGate-campaignOwner-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
        return False
    rec_owners = _owners_of(record.get("owner"))
    if not rec_owners.intersection(set(owners)):
        missing.append("campaign-owner-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    stage = stages.get(gate_stage)
    if stage is None:
        missing.append("action-stage-missing:" + gate_stage)
        return False
    if stage.get("status") == "blocked":
        missing.append("action-stage-blocked:" + gate_stage)
        return False
    record_id = stage.get("campaignRecordId")
    if type(record_id) is not str or record_id == "":
        missing.append("stage-campaignRecordId-null:" + gate_stage)
        return False
    if record_id != campaign_id:
        missing.append("stage-campaignRecordId-mismatch:" + gate_stage)
        return False
    rec_profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
    stage_profile = stage.get("acceptedProfile")
    status = record.get("status")
    complete = _campaign_is_complete(status)
    if complete:
        if stage.get("status") != "complete":
            missing.append("action-stage-incomplete:" + gate_stage)
            return False
        if type(rec_profile) is not str or rec_profile == "":
            missing.append("acceptedProfile-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        if type(stage_profile) is not str or stage_profile == "":
            missing.append("stage-acceptedProfile-unresolved:" + gate_stage)
            return False
        if rec_profile != stage_profile:
            missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
            return False
    elif (
        type(rec_profile) is str
        and type(stage_profile) is str
        and rec_profile != stage_profile
    ):
        missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if action["evidenceProfile"] not in EVIDENCE_PROFILES:
        missing.append("evidenceProfile-unsupported:" + action["evidenceProfile"])
        return False
    if not _evidence_applicable(action["evidenceProfile"], status):
        missing.append("evidenceProfile-inapplicable:" + action["evidenceProfile"])
        return False
    rec_hash = record.get("candidateHash")
    if rec_hash is None:
        rec_hash = record.get("candidateSha256")
    stage_hash = stage.get("candidateHash")
    action_hash = action["candidate"]
    if complete:
        if type(rec_hash) is not str or rec_hash == "":
            missing.append("candidateHash-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        if type(stage_hash) is not str or stage_hash == "":
            missing.append("stage-candidateHash-null:" + gate_stage)
            return False
    if type(rec_hash) is str and action_hash.lower() != rec_hash.lower():
        missing.append("action-candidate-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(stage_hash) is str and action_hash.lower() != stage_hash.lower():
        missing.append("stage-candidate-conflict:" + gate_stage)
        return False
    texts = [
        str(stage.get("purpose") or ""),
        str(record.get("scope") or ""),
        str(action["requirement"]),
    ]
    if not _capability_agrees(action["capability"], texts):
        missing.append("action-capability-unresolved:" + action["capability"])
        return False
    if not _status_allows(status, action["kind"]):
        missing.append("campaign-status-unsupported:" + str(status))
        return False
    return True


def _evidence_applicable(profile, status):
    if profile not in EVIDENCE_PROFILES:
        return False
    rank = EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index(profile)
    if type(status) is not str or status == "":
        return False
    if _campaign_is_complete(status):
        return rank <= EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index("syntax")
    if status.startswith("admitted-"):
        return rank <= EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index("local-runtime")
    return False


def _capability_agrees(capability, texts):
    if type(capability) is not str or capability == "":
        return False
    blob = " ".join(texts).lower().replace("/", " ").replace("_", "-")
    for stem in capability.lower().replace("_", "-").split("-"):
        if stem == "" or stem in STOP_WORDS:
            continue
        if stem not in blob:
            return False
    return True


def _owners_of(value):
    if type(value) is not str:
        return set()
    parts = set()
    for chunk in value.replace(",", "/").split("/"):
        item = chunk.strip()
        if item:
            parts.add(item)
    return parts


def _status_allows(status, kind):
    if type(status) is not str or status == "":
        return False
    if status == "complete-but-revoked":
        return False
    if kind == "report":
        return True
    if kind in ("review", "reproduce", "verify"):
        return status == "complete" or status.startswith("admitted-")
    if kind in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
        return status.startswith("admitted-")
    return False


def _verify_binding(root, campaign_id, record, missing, expected_stage=None):
    binding_rel = record.get("binding")
    expected = record.get("bindingSha256")
    if binding_rel is None and expected is None:
        missing.append("binding-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    if type(binding_rel) is not str or type(expected) is not str:
        missing.append("binding-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    path = _contained_file(root, binding_rel, missing, "binding")
    if path is None:
        missing.append("binding-missing:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    data = _read_bytes(path, missing, binding_rel)
    if data is None:
        return False, None
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    if digest != expected.lower():
        missing.append("bindingSha256-stale:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    payload = _parse_json(data, missing, binding_rel)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("binding-not-object:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    if payload == {}:
        missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:empty")
        return False, None
    schema = payload.get("schema")
    if type(schema) is not str:
        missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:" + type(schema).__name__)
        return False, None
    if schema != BINDING_SCHEMA:
        missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:" + schema)
        return False, None
    for key in ("status", "authority", "scope", "resources"):
        if key not in payload:
            missing.append("binding-field-missing:" + key)
            return False, None
        if key != "resources" and type(payload[key]) is not str:
            missing.append("binding-field-type:" + key)
            return False, None
    if payload.get("status") != "admitted-design-subset-only":
        missing.append("binding-status-unsupported:" + str(payload.get("status")))
        return False, None
    if _contained_file(root, payload["authority"], missing, "binding-authority") is None:
        return False, None
    resources = payload["resources"]
    if type(resources) is not dict:
        missing.append("binding-resources-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    seconds = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
    if seconds is not None and type(seconds) is not int:
        missing.append("binding-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    if not _verify_path_map(root, payload.get("inputs"), missing, "binding-input"):
        return False, None
    owned = payload.get("ownedFiles")
    if owned is None:
        pass
    elif type(owned) is list:
        for item in owned:
            if type(item) is not str:
                missing.append("binding-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
                return False, None
            if _contained_file(root, item, missing, "binding-owned") is None:
                return False, None
    elif type(owned) is dict:
        if not _verify_path_map(root, owned, missing, "binding-owned"):
            return False, None
    else:
        missing.append("binding-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False, None
    if not _binding_stage_and_profile(
        root, payload, record, expected_stage, campaign_id, missing
    ):
        return False, None
    return True, payload


def _binding_stage_and_profile(
    root, payload, record, expected_stage, campaign_id, missing
):
    complete = _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status"))
    if not complete:
        return True
    bind_stage = payload.get("stage")
    rec_stage = record.get("stage")
    if type(bind_stage) is not str or bind_stage == "":
        missing.append("binding-stage-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(rec_stage) is not str or rec_stage == "" or bind_stage != rec_stage:
        missing.append("binding-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(expected_stage) is str and expected_stage != "" and bind_stage != expected_stage:
        missing.append("binding-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    derived = _bounds_profile(root, payload, missing, campaign_id)
    if derived is None:
        missing.append("acceptedProfile-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    rec_profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
    if rec_profile != derived:
        missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    return True


def _bounds_profile(root, payload, missing, campaign_id):
    mapping = payload.get("inputs")
    if type(mapping) is not dict:
        return None
    found = None
    for rel in mapping:
        if type(rel) is not str or rel == "":
            continue
        path = _contained_file(root, rel, missing, "binding-bounds")
        if path is None:
            return None
        data = _read_bytes(path, missing, rel)
        if data is None:
            return None
        parsed = _optional_object(data)
        if parsed is None or parsed.get("schemaVersion") != BOUNDS_SCHEMA:
            continue
        profile = parsed.get("semanticProfile")
        if type(profile) is not str or profile == "":
            missing.append("binding-profile-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        if found is not None and found != profile:
            missing.append("binding-profile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        found = profile
    return found


def _optional_object(data):
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8"), object_pairs_hook=_object_pairs)
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, DuplicateKeyError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None
    if type(parsed) is dict:
        return parsed
    return None


def _verify_path_map(root, mapping, missing, kind):
    if mapping is None:
        return True
    if type(mapping) is not dict:
        missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
        return False
    for rel, digest in mapping.items():
        if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
            missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
            return False
        path = _contained_file(root, rel, missing, kind)
        if path is None:
            return False
        data = _read_bytes(path, missing, rel)
        if data is None:
            return False
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest.lower():
            missing.append(kind + "-stale:" + rel)
            return False
    return True


def _verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, record, missing):
    manifest_rel = record.get("candidateManifest")
    if type(manifest_rel) is not str or manifest_rel == "":
        missing.append("candidateManifest-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    man_path = _contained_file(root, manifest_rel, missing, "candidateManifest")
    if man_path is None:
        return False
    manifest = _load_json_path(man_path, missing, manifest_rel)
    if type(manifest) is not dict:
        missing.append("candidateManifest-not-object:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    extra = [key for key in manifest if key not in MANIFEST_KEYS]
    for key in extra:
        missing.append("unknown-manifest-field:" + key)
        return False
    if "schema" in manifest:
        missing.append("candidateManifest-schema-unresolved:" + str(manifest.get("schema")))
        return False
    owned = manifest.get("ownedFiles")
    if type(owned) is not dict or not owned:
        missing.append("candidate-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    for rel, digest in owned.items():
        if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
            missing.append("candidate-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
    algo = manifest.get("digestAlgorithm")
    candidate_sha = manifest.get("candidateSha256")
    if type(candidate_sha) is not str or candidate_sha == "":
        missing.append("candidateSha256-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(algo) is not str or "SHA256" not in algo:
        missing.append("digestAlgorithm-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    compact = json.dumps(owned, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
    if hashlib.sha256(compact.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != candidate_sha.lower():
        missing.append("candidateSha256-stale:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    record_hash = record.get("candidateHash")
    if record_hash is not None and type(record_hash) is not str:
        missing.append("candidateHash-type:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(record_hash) is str and record_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
        missing.append("candidateHash-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    inputs = manifest.get("inputs")
    if type(inputs) is not dict:
        missing.append("candidate-inputs-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    review_rel = record.get("review")
    if type(review_rel) is str and review_rel != "":
        if not _apply_review(root, review_rel, record, candidate_sha, campaign_id, missing):
            return False
    elif _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status")):
        missing.append("review-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    published_skip = None
    acc_rel = record.get("acceptance")
    if type(acc_rel) is str and acc_rel != "":
        published_skip = _apply_acceptance(
            root, acc_rel, man_path, candidate_sha, owned, campaign_id, missing, expected_review=record.get("review")
        )
        if published_skip is False:
            return False
    elif _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status")):
        missing.append("acceptance-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
    if profile is not None and type(profile) is not str:
        missing.append("acceptedProfile-type:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    freeze_bytes = {
        rel: digest
        for rel, digest in owned.items()
        if rel != published_skip
    }
    if not _verify_path_map(root, freeze_bytes, missing, "candidate-source"):
        return False
    return _verify_path_map(root, inputs, missing, "candidate-input")


def _freeze_archive_rel(manifest_rel, published_rel):
    posix = manifest_rel.replace("\\", "/")
    published = published_rel.replace("\\", "/")
    if not posix.endswith("-freeze.json"):
        return None
    parts = [part for part in posix.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
    if not parts:
        return None
    stem = parts[-1][: -len("-freeze.json")]
    if stem == "":
        return None
    archived = parts[:-1] + [stem] + [part for part in published.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
    if any(part == ".." for part in archived):
        return None
    return "/".join(archived)


def _publication_core(payload):
    core = json.loads(json.dumps(payload))
    subsets = core.get("subsets")
    if type(subsets) is not dict:
        return core
    subset = subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET)
    if type(subset) is not dict:
        return core
    for key in PUBLICATION_MUTABLE:
        subset.pop(key, None)
    outputs = subset.get("outputs")
    if type(outputs) is list:
        for item in outputs:
            if type(item) is dict:
                item.pop("sha256", None)
                item.pop("status", None)
    summaries = subset.get("rowSummaries")
    if type(summaries) is dict:
        for row in summaries.values():
            if type(row) is dict:
                row.pop("closureTask", None)
    return core


def _publication_transform_ok(
    root,
    original_bytes,
    current_bytes,
    owned,
    candidate_sha,
    acceptance,
    published_path,
    missing,
):
    original = _parse_json(original_bytes, missing, "publication-original")
    current = _parse_json(current_bytes, missing, published_path)
    if type(original) is not dict or type(current) is not dict:
        missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
        return False
    if original.get("schemaVersion") != PUBLICATION_SCHEMA:
        missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
        return False
    if current.get("schemaVersion") != original.get("schemaVersion"):
        missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
        return False
    if _publication_core(original) != _publication_core(current):
        missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
        return False
    subsets = current.get("subsets")
    subset = subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET) if type(subsets) is dict else None
    if type(subset) is not dict:
        missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
        return False
    if subset.get("status") != PUBLICATION_STATUS:
        missing.append("publication-control-status:" + published_path)
        return False
    digest = subset.get("candidateHash")
    if type(digest) is not str or digest.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
        missing.append("publication-control-candidateHash:" + published_path)
        return False
    bound = subset.get("candidateHashStatus")
    if bound != PUBLICATION_HASH_STATUS:
        missing.append("publication-control-candidateHashStatus:" + published_path)
        return False
    if not _publication_acceptance_ok(
        subset.get("acceptance"), acceptance, root, published_path, missing
    ):
        return False
    if not _publication_reviews_ok(
        root, subset.get("reviews"), acceptance, published_path, missing, candidate_sha=candidate_sha
    ):
        return False
    outputs = subset.get("outputs")
    if type(outputs) is not list:
        missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
        return False
    for item in outputs:
        if type(item) is not dict:
            missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
            return False
        path = item.get("path")
        digest = item.get("sha256")
        if type(path) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
            missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
            return False
        if item.get("status") != PUBLICATION_OUTPUT_STATUS:
            missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
            return False
        frozen = owned.get(path)
        if type(frozen) is not str or frozen.lower() != digest.lower():
            missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
            return False
    orig_subsets = original.get("subsets")
    orig_sub = orig_subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET) if type(orig_subsets) is dict else None
    if not _publication_closures_ok(orig_sub, subset, published_path, missing):
        return False
    return True


def _publication_acceptance_ok(pub_acc, acceptance, root, published_path, missing):
    if type(pub_acc) is not dict:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    extra = [key for key in pub_acc if key not in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS]
    absent = [key for key in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS if key not in pub_acc]
    if extra or absent:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    for key in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS:
        if type(pub_acc.get(key)) is not str or pub_acc[key] == "":
            missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
            return False
    if pub_acc["scope"] != DESIGN_SCOPE:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if pub_acc["candidateManifest"] != acceptance.get("sourceCandidate"):
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if pub_acc["review"] != acceptance.get("review"):
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if pub_acc["networkMilestones"] != PUBLICATION_NETWORK:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if pub_acc["metadataRule"] != PUBLICATION_METADATA:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if pub_acc["acceptedAtomicCode"] != "c30aaf5c0d091b1efea7e042d0fcbd539ca6cc51":
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    rec_at = pub_acc.get("recordedAt")
    try:
        datetime.fromisoformat(rec_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except Exception:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    static_ver = pub_acc.get("staticVerification")
    if (
        type(static_ver) is not str
        or os.path.normpath(static_ver)
        != "evidence/moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07/SP01/loan-swap-subset-01/verify3-receipt.json"
    ):
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    stat_file = _contained_file(root, static_ver, missing, "publication-static")
    if stat_file is None:
        return False
    body = _read_bytes(stat_file, missing, static_ver)
    if body is None:
        return False
    if (
        hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()
        != "eeef0e21216de0348ac96250508f38c2fc89d657c63176a822a7f6c8e0f3c8f2"
    ):
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    receipt_data = _parse_json(body, missing, static_ver)
    if type(receipt_data) is not dict:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if receipt_data.get("kind") != "verify3":
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if receipt_data.get("exitCode") != 0:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if (
        receipt_data.get("scope")
        != "SP01 loan/swap design subset only. No proof, ledger or full RP01 acceptance."
    ):
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if receipt_data.get("unauthorizedGitActivity") is not False:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    if receipt_data.get("storageStop") is not False:
        missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
        return False
    return True


def _publication_reviews_ok(root, reviews, acceptance, published_path, missing, candidate_sha=""):
    if type(reviews) is not list or reviews == []:
        missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
        return False
    matched = False
    wanted = acceptance.get("review")
    for item in reviews:
        if type(item) is not dict:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        extra = [key for key in item if key not in PUBLICATION_REVIEW_ITEM_KEYS]
        if extra:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        path = item.get("path")
        digest = item.get("sha256")
        verdict = item.get("verdict")
        if type(path) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if type(verdict) is not str or verdict != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if item.get("scope") != DESIGN_SCOPE:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        file_path = _contained_file(root, path, missing, "publication-review")
        if file_path is None:
            return False
        body = _read_bytes(file_path, missing, path)
        if body is None:
            return False
        if hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest() != digest.lower():
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        review_obj = _parse_json(body, missing, path)
        if type(review_obj) is not dict:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if review_obj.get("verdict") != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if review_obj.get("scope") != DESIGN_SCOPE:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if candidate_sha and str(review_obj.get("candidateSha256", "")).lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
        if path == wanted:
            matched = True
        else:
            missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
            return False
    if not matched:
        missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
        return False
    return True


def _publication_closures_ok(orig_sub, subset, published_path, missing):
    summaries = subset.get("rowSummaries")
    orig_rows = orig_sub.get("rowSummaries") if type(orig_sub) is dict else None
    if type(summaries) is not dict or type(orig_rows) is not dict:
        missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
        return False
    if set(summaries.keys()) != set(orig_rows.keys()):
        missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
        return False
    for key, row in summaries.items():
        if type(row) is not dict:
            missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
            return False
        orig_row = orig_rows.get(key)
        if type(orig_row) is not dict:
            missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
            return False
        if "closureTask" not in row or "closureTask" not in orig_row:
            missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
            return False
        current = row["closureTask"]
        original = orig_row["closureTask"]
        if type(current) is not str or type(original) is not str:
            missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
            return False
        if not _closure_allowed(original, current):
            missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
            return False
    return True


def _closure_allowed(original, current):
    if type(current) is not str or type(original) is not str:
        return False
    if current == original:
        return True
    if CLOSURE_PENDING not in original:
        return False
    expected = original.replace(CLOSURE_PENDING, CLOSURE_DESIGN, 1)
    return current == expected


def _apply_acceptance(root, acc_rel, man_path, candidate_sha, owned, campaign_id, missing, expected_review=None):
    acc_path = _contained_file(root, acc_rel, missing, "acceptance")
    if acc_path is None:
        return False
    acceptance = _load_json_path(acc_path, missing, acc_rel)
    if type(acceptance) is not dict:
        missing.append("acceptance-not-object:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    extra = [key for key in acceptance if key not in ACCEPTANCE_KEYS]
    for key in extra:
        missing.append("unknown-acceptance-field:" + key)
        return False
    for field in ACCEPTANCE_REQUIRED:
        value = acceptance.get(field)
        if type(value) is not str or value == "":
            missing.append("acceptance-field-unresolved:" + field)
            return False
    status = acceptance.get("status")
    if not _acceptance_is_complete(status):
        missing.append("acceptance-status-unsupported:" + str(status))
        return False
    acc_hash = acceptance.get("candidateHash")
    if acc_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
        missing.append("acceptance-candidateHash-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if expected_review is not None and acceptance.get("review") != expected_review:
        missing.append("acceptance-review-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    source = acceptance.get("sourceCandidate")
    src_path = _contained_file(root, source, missing, "sourceCandidate")
    if src_path is None or src_path != man_path:
        missing.append("acceptance-sourceCandidate-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    unchanged = acceptance.get("unchangedPayloadHashes")
    if unchanged is None:
        unchanged = {}
    if type(unchanged) is not dict:
        missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    for rel, digest in unchanged.items():
        if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
            missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        frozen = owned.get(rel)
        if type(frozen) is not str or frozen.lower() != digest.lower():
            missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-conflict:" + rel)
            return False
    published_path = acceptance.get("publishedControlPath")
    published_sha = acceptance.get("publishedControlSha256")
    frozen_digest = owned.get(published_path)
    if type(frozen_digest) is not str or frozen_digest == "":
        missing.append("publication-bridge-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    pub_path = _contained_file(root, published_path, missing, "published-control")
    if pub_path is None:
        missing.append("publication-bridge-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    data = _read_bytes(pub_path, missing, published_path)
    if data is None:
        return False
    current_digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    if current_digest != published_sha.lower():
        missing.append("publication-bridge-stale:" + published_path)
        return False
    if current_digest == frozen_digest.lower():
        return None
    archive_rel = _freeze_archive_rel(source, published_path)
    if archive_rel is None:
        missing.append("publication-original-unavailable:" + published_path)
        return False
    archive_path = _contained_file(root, archive_rel, missing, "publication-original")
    if archive_path is None:
        missing.append("publication-original-unavailable:" + published_path)
        return False
    original = _read_bytes(archive_path, missing, archive_rel)
    if original is None:
        return False
    if hashlib.sha256(original).hexdigest() != frozen_digest.lower():
        missing.append("publication-original-stale:" + archive_rel)
        return False
    if not _publication_transform_ok(
        root,
        original,
        data,
        owned,
        candidate_sha,
        acceptance,
        published_path,
        missing,
    ):
        return False
    return published_path


def _apply_review(root, review_rel, record, candidate_sha, campaign_id, missing):
    rev_path = _contained_file(root, review_rel, missing, "review")
    if rev_path is None:
        return False
    review = _load_json_path(rev_path, missing, review_rel)
    if type(review) is not dict:
        missing.append("review-not-object:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    extra = [key for key in review if key not in REVIEW_KEYS]
    for key in extra:
        missing.append("unknown-review-field:" + key)
        return False
    for field in REVIEW_REQUIRED:
        value = review.get(field)
        if type(value) is not str or value == "":
            missing.append("review-field-unresolved:" + field)
            return False
    findings = review.get("findings")
    if type(findings) is not list:
        missing.append("review-findings-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    rev_hash = review.get("candidateSha256")
    if rev_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
        missing.append("review-candidateSha256-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    rev_scope = review.get("scope")
    camp_scope = record.get("scope")
    if rev_scope != DESIGN_SCOPE:
        missing.append("review-scope-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if type(camp_scope) is str and rev_scope != camp_scope:
        missing.append("review-scope-conflict:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if record.get("stage") != "rp01-mc02":
        missing.append("review-scope-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    verdict = review.get("verdict")
    if verdict != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
        if type(verdict) is str and verdict.upper() in REVIEW_VERDICT_FAIL:
            missing.append("review-verdict-blocked:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        missing.append("review-verdict-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    reviewer = review.get("reviewer")
    lowered = reviewer.lower()
    if lowered.startswith("author") or "author-" in lowered:
        missing.append("review-reviewer-not-independent:" + reviewer)
        return False
    return True


def _verify_resource(root, campaign_id, record, program, binding_obj, action, missing):
    relative = record.get("resourceAmendment")
    if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
        missing.append("resourceAmendment-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, "resource")
    if path is None:
        missing.append("resourceAmendment-missing:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("resourceAmendment-not-object:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if payload == {}:
        missing.append("resource-empty:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    extra_amendment = [k for k in payload if k not in RESOURCE_AMENDMENT_KEYS]
    if extra_amendment:
        for k in extra_amendment:
            missing.append("unknown-resourceAmendment-field:" + k)
        return False
    schema = payload.get("schema")
    if schema is not None and schema not in RESOURCE_SCHEMAS:
        missing.append("resource-schema-unresolved:" + str(schema))
        return False
    authority = payload.get("authority")
    if type(authority) is not str or authority == "":
        missing.append("resource-authority-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    if _contained_file(root, authority, missing, "resource-authority") is None:
        return False
    alloc = _amendment_seconds(payload, campaign_id, missing)
    if alloc is None:
        return False
    res_scope = payload.get("scope")
    camp_scope = record.get("scope")
    if res_scope is not None:
        if type(res_scope) is not str or res_scope == "":
            missing.append("resource-scope-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        if type(camp_scope) is str and res_scope != camp_scope:
            missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
            return False
    successors = record.get("successorAmendments")
    if successors is None:
        successors = []
    if type(successors) is not list:
        missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    for item in successors:
        if type(item) is not str:
            missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return False
        extra = _load_amendment_file(root, item, campaign_id, missing)
        if extra is None:
            return False
        alloc += extra
    if type(binding_obj) is dict:
        resources = binding_obj.get("resources")
        if type(resources) is dict:
            bound = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
            if bound is not None:
                if type(bound) is not int:
                    missing.append("binding-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
                    return False
                alloc += bound
    if alloc <= 0:
        missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    historical = None
    if type(program) is dict:
        runtime = program.get("resourceRuntimeSnapshot")
        if type(runtime) is dict:
            historical = runtime.get("path")
    spec = record.get("currentAccounting")
    if spec is None:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    remaining = _remaining_from_current(
        root, spec, record, binding_obj, action, alloc, historical, campaign_id, missing
    )
    if remaining is None:
        return False
    if remaining < 0:
        missing.append("resource-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
        return False
    return True


def _amendment_seconds(payload, campaign_id, missing):
    if "allocationSeconds" in payload:
        value = payload["allocationSeconds"]
        if type(value) is not int or value <= 0:
            missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
        if additional is None:
            return value
        if type(additional) is not int or additional < 0:
            missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        return value + additional
    additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
    if type(additional) is not int or additional <= 0:
        missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    return additional


def _load_amendment_file(root, relative, campaign_id, missing):
    path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, "successor-amendment")
    if path is None:
        return None
    payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    authority = payload.get("authority")
    if type(authority) is str and authority != "":
        if _contained_file(root, authority, missing, "successor-authority") is None:
            return None
    additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
    if type(additional) is not int or additional < 0:
        missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    return additional


def _remaining_from_current(
    root, spec, record, binding_obj, action, alloc, historical, campaign_id, missing
):
    if type(spec) is not dict:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    extra = [key for key in spec if key not in CURRENT_ACCOUNTING_KEYS]
    for key in extra:
        missing.append("unknown-currentAccounting-field:" + key)
        return None
    if spec.get("schema") != ACCOUNTING_SCHEMA:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    relative = spec.get("path")
    if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if type(historical) is str and os.path.normpath(relative) == os.path.normpath(historical):
        missing.append("resource-historical-snapshot:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, "current-accounting")
    if path is None:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    extra_acc = [key for key in payload if key not in ACCOUNTING_KEYS]
    for key in extra_acc:
        missing.append("unknown-accounting-field:" + key)
        return None
    if payload.get("schema") != ACCOUNTING_SCHEMA:
        missing.append("resource-snapshot-schema-unresolved:" + str(payload.get("schema")))
        return None
    auth_sha = payload.get("authority_sha256")
    if type(auth_sha) is not str or auth_sha.lower() != "f6b7f17d1bda437d5f83abc656ccf4a0d9c7a57a83995668387c29bd0328f492":
        missing.append("resource-authority-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    planning = _nonneg_number(payload.get("planning_charge_seconds"), True)
    overhead = _nonneg_number(payload.get("planning_overhead_reserved_seconds"), True)
    if planning is None or overhead is None:
        missing.append("resource-planning-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    master_limit = payload.get("master_limit_seconds")
    package_limit = payload.get("package_limit_seconds")
    if type(master_limit) is not int or master_limit <= 0:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if type(package_limit) is not int or package_limit <= 0:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    package = payload.get("package")
    rec_owners = _owners_of(record.get("owner"))
    if type(package) is not str or package == "":
        missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if rec_owners and package not in rec_owners:
        missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    charges = _seconds_list(payload.get("charges"), missing, campaign_id)
    reserved = _seconds_list(payload.get("reserved"), missing, campaign_id)
    external = _seconds_list(payload.get("externalPackageCharges"), missing, campaign_id)
    if charges is None or reserved is None or external is None:
        return None
    master_used = planning + overhead + charges + reserved + external
    package_used = planning + overhead + charges
    pass_binding = _optional_pass_record(
        root, spec.get("passBinding"), PASS_BINDING_OPTIONAL, missing, "pass-binding"
    )
    if pass_binding is False:
        return None
    if type(pass_binding) is dict:
        if pass_binding.get("schema") == "moriarty.plugin-focused-source-pass/1" and record.get("stage") != "plugin":
            missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        pb_scope = pass_binding.get("scope")
        rec_scope = record.get("scope")
        if type(pb_scope) is not str or (rec_scope and pb_scope != rec_scope and pass_binding.get("schema") != "moriarty.plugin-focused-source-pass/1"):
            missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        pb_worktree = pass_binding.get("worktree")
        if type(pb_worktree) is not str or os.path.normpath(pb_worktree) != os.path.normpath(str(root)):
            missing.append("resource-worktree-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        pb_parent = pass_binding.get("parentCandidate")
        if type(pb_parent) is not str or pb_parent != record.get("candidateHash"):
            missing.append("resource-candidate-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
            return None
    pass_ledger = _optional_pass_record(
        root, spec.get("passLedger"), PASS_LEDGER_KEYS, missing, "pass-ledger"
    )
    if pass_ledger is False:
        return None
    if type(pass_ledger) is dict and pass_ledger.get("stopped") is True:
        missing.append("resource-pass-stopped:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    cost = _action_cost(action, binding_obj, pass_binding, campaign_id, missing)
    if cost is None:
        return None
    prepaid = False
    runners = binding_obj.get("runners", {}) if type(binding_obj) is dict else {}
    if type(runners) is dict and action["id"] in runners:
        # A command declaration cannot create credit. Resolve the campaign's
        # hash-bound runner and its exact existing debit before recognizing it.
        # load_runner checks source/debit identity without calling this reader.
        try:
            from moriarty_dev.runner import load_runner
            plan, _ = load_runner(root, action)
            if not cost <= plan["chargedSeconds"] <= alloc:
                raise ValueError("debit does not match the admitted action bound")
            prepaid = True
        except Exception as exc:
            missing.append("resource-runner-debit-unresolved:" + str(exc))
            return None
    envelope_remain = None
    if type(pass_binding) is dict:
        envelope_id = pass_binding.get("envelope")
        envelopes = payload.get("successorEnvelopes")
        if type(envelope_id) is str and envelope_id != "":
            if type(envelopes) is not dict or envelope_id not in envelopes:
                missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
                return None
            env = envelopes[envelope_id]
            if type(env) is not dict:
                missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
                return None
            envelope_remain = env.get("remainingReservedSeconds")
            if type(envelope_remain) is not int:
                missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
                return None
            env_dispatches = env.get("workerDispatches")
            env_limit = env.get("workerDispatchLimit")
            if action["kind"] in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
                if type(env_dispatches) is not int or type(env_limit) is not int:
                    missing.append("resource-dispatch-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
                    return None
                if env_dispatches >= env_limit:
                    missing.append("resource-dispatch-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
                    return None
    if action["kind"] in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
        dispatches = payload.get("worker_dispatches")
        dispatch_limit = payload.get("worker_dispatch_limit")
        if type(dispatches) is not int or type(dispatch_limit) is not int:
            missing.append("resource-dispatch-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        if dispatches >= dispatch_limit:
            missing.append("resource-dispatch-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
            return None
    remainders = [
        master_limit - master_used,
        package_limit - package_used,
        alloc,
    ]
    if type(envelope_remain) is int:
        remainders.append(envelope_remain)
    # The existing debit already contributes to master_used/package_used.
    remaining = min(remainders) - (0 if prepaid else cost)
    return remaining


def _nonneg_number(value, required):
    if value is None:
        return None if required else 0
    if type(value) is bool:
        return None
    if type(value) is int:
        return value if value >= 0 else None
    if type(value) is float:
        if value != value or value in (float("inf"), float("-inf")):
            return None
        return value if value >= 0 else None
    return None


def _seconds_list(rows, missing, campaign_id):
    if rows is None or type(rows) is not list:
        missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    total = 0
    for row in rows:
        if type(row) is not dict:
            missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        seconds = row.get("seconds")
        if type(seconds) is bool or type(seconds) is not int or seconds < 0:
            missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
            return None
        total += seconds
    return total


def _optional_pass_record(root, relative, allowed, missing, kind):
    if relative is None:
        return None
    if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
        missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
        return False
    path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, kind)
    if path is None:
        return False
    payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append(kind + "-not-object")
        return False
    extra = [key for key in payload if key not in allowed]
    for key in extra:
        missing.append("unknown-" + kind + "-field:" + key)
        return False
    return payload


PASS_BINDING_OPTIONAL = (
    "authority",
    "base",
    "envelope",
    "owned",
    "parentCandidate",
    "parentFreezeSha256",
    "resources",
    "runtimeNodeModules",
    "schema",
    "scope",
    "sources",
    "worktree",
    "allocationSeconds",
    "workerDispatches",
    "retainedBytes",
    "ownedBytesLimit",
    "verificationCommands",
)


def _action_cost(action, binding_obj, pass_binding, campaign_id, missing):
    kind = action["kind"]
    if kind == "report":
        return 0
    resources = {}
    if type(binding_obj) is dict and type(binding_obj.get("resources")) is dict:
        resources = binding_obj["resources"]
    if type(pass_binding) is dict and type(pass_binding.get("resources")) is dict:
        merged = dict(resources)
        merged.update(pass_binding["resources"])
        resources = merged
    commands = resources.get("verificationCommands")
    if type(commands) is not dict:
        commands = {}
    if kind == "review":
        value = commands.get("review")
        if type(value) is int and value > 0:
            return value
        gpt = resources.get("gpt6ReviewSeconds")
        if type(gpt) is int and gpt > 0:
            return gpt
        missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if kind == "verify":
        value = commands.get("verify")
        if type(value) is int and value > 0:
            return value
        verify = resources.get("verificationSeconds")
        if type(verify) is int and verify > 0:
            return verify
        missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if kind == "reproduce":
        value = commands.get("reproduce")
        if type(value) is int and value > 0:
            return value
        verify = resources.get("verificationSeconds")
        if type(verify) is int and verify > 0:
            return verify
        missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    if kind in IMPLEMENT_KINDS or kind == "admin":
        workers = resources.get("workerSeconds")
        if type(workers) is list and workers and type(workers[0]) is int and workers[0] > 0:
            return workers[0]
        bound = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
        if type(bound) is int and bound > 0:
            return bound
        missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
        return None
    missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
    return None


def _resolve_file_admission(root, ref, missing):
    if ref == "":
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:")
        return False, False, False
    path = _contained_file(root, ref, missing, "admissionRef")
    if path is None:
        return False, False, False
    payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, ref)
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved-shape:" + ref)
        return False, False, False
    schema = payload.get("schema")
    missing.append("admissionRef-unsupported-schema:" + str(schema))
    return False, False, False


def _select_next(
    root,
    actions,
    current,
    history_fields,
    history_reader,
    program,
    sprints,
    campaigns,
    program_ok,
    sprints_ok,
    campaign_ok,
    missing,
):
    ranked = sorted(
        actions,
        key=lambda row: (
            NEXT_KIND_ORDER.index(row["kind"])
            if row["kind"] in NEXT_KIND_ORDER
            else 99,
            row["id"],
        ),
    )
    history_missing = [
        item
        for item in missing
        if item == "operational-history" or item.startswith("operational-history")
    ]
    for other in ranked:
        local = []
        flags = _action_flags(
            root,
            other,
            program,
            sprints,
            campaigns,
            program_ok,
            sprints_ok,
            campaign_ok,
            local,
        )
        snap = _snapshot(**flags, missingEvidence=list(local))
        same_lineage = (
            other["requirement"] == current["requirement"]
            and other["capability"] == current["capability"]
        )
        if same_lineage:
            snap.update(history_fields)
            for item in history_missing:
                if item not in local:
                    local.append(item)
        else:
            _apply_history(history_reader, root, other, snap, local)
        snap["missingEvidence"] = list(local)
        snap["nextActionId"] = None
        decision = assess(snap, other)
        if decision.get("allow") is True:
            return other["id"]
    missing.append("next-action-none")
    return None


def _apply_history(history_reader, root, action, snap, missing):
    if history_reader is None:
        missing.append("operational-history")
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    if not callable(history_reader):
        missing.append("operational-history-not-callable")
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    lineage = {
        "repository": _repository_identity(root, missing),
        "requirement": action["requirement"],
        "capability": action["capability"],
    }
    try:
        payload = history_reader(lineage)
    except Exception as exc:
        missing.append("operational-history-error:" + type(exc).__name__)
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    if payload is None:
        missing.append("operational-history")
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    if type(payload) is not dict:
        missing.append("operational-history-invalid")
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    extra = [key for key in payload if key not in HISTORY_KEYS]
    if extra:
        missing.append("operational-history-invalid")
        snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
        snap["adminCycles"] = None
        snap["adminSeconds"] = None
        return
    for key in HISTORY_INT_KEYS:
        value = payload.get(key)
        if value is None:
            snap[key] = None
            continue
        if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
            missing.append("operational-history-type:" + key)
            return
        snap[key] = value
    for key in HISTORY_BOOL_KEYS:
        value = payload.get(key)
        if type(value) is not bool:
            missing.append("operational-history-type:" + key)
            return
        snap[key] = value


def _repository_identity(root, missing):
    repo_path = Path(root).resolve()
    git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
    try:
        if git_entry.is_file():
            text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
                gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
                if not gitdir.is_absolute():
                    gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
                commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
                if commondir_file.is_file():
                    cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                    common = Path(cd_text)
                    if not common.is_absolute():
                        common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
                    return str(common)
                return str(gitdir)
        elif git_entry.is_dir():
            return str(git_entry.resolve())
    except Exception:
        pass
    missing.append("git-common-dir-unresolved")
    return str(repo_path)


def _contained_file(root, relative, missing, kind):
    if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
        missing.append(kind + "-path-empty")
        return None
    posix = relative.replace("\\", "/")
    if posix.startswith("/") or _has_drive(posix):
        missing.append(kind + "-path-not-contained:" + relative)
        return None
    parts = [part for part in posix.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
    if any(part == ".." for part in parts):
        missing.append("path-escape:" + relative)
        return None
    if _secret_parts(parts):
        missing.append("secret-path-refused:" + relative)
        return None
    candidate = root.joinpath(*parts)
    try:
        resolved = candidate.resolve()
    except OSError:
        missing.append(kind + "-path-unresolved:" + relative)
        return None
    if not resolved.is_relative_to(root):
        missing.append("path-containment-rejected-symlink:" + relative)
        return None
    try:
        resolved_rel = resolved.relative_to(root)
        if _secret_parts(resolved_rel.parts):
            missing.append("secret-path-refused:" + relative)
            return None
    except ValueError:
        missing.append("path-containment-rejected:" + relative)
        return None
    if not resolved.is_file():
        missing.append(kind + "-missing:" + relative)
        return None
    return resolved


def _secret_parts(parts):
    for part in parts:
        lowered = part.lower()
        for secret in SECRET_PARTS:
            if lowered == secret or lowered.startswith(secret + "."):
                return True
            if secret in lowered.split("."):
                return True
            if secret in lowered and secret in ("wallet", "seed", "witness"):
                return True
    return False


def _has_drive(path):
    return len(path) >= 2 and path[1] == ":" and path[0].isalpha()


def _structural_block(missing):
    markers = (
        "duplicate-",
        "unknown-",
        "schemaVersion-",
        "sprints-schemaVersion",
        "actions-schema-",
        "secret-path-refused:",
        "path-escape:",
        "path-containment-",
        "binding-schema-",
        "campaign-schema-",
        "entryGate-requires-",
        "commandRef-",
        "unsupported-",
        "unknown-nested-",
        "unknown-actions-field:",
        "unknown-command-field:",
        "unknown-stageAdmission-field:",
        "unknown-entryGate-field:",
        "unknown-program-field:",
        "unknown-campaign-field:",
        "unknown-manifest-field:",
        "unknown-acceptance-field:",
        "unknown-review-field:",
        "unknown-accounting-field:",
        "stageAdmission-schema-",
        "resource-schema-unresolved:",
        "candidateManifest-schema-",
    )
    return any(item.startswith(markers) for item in missing)


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/runner.py
"""Adapter for a campaign-bound, already charged Foreman invocation.

Campaign binding bytes authorize the command. The existing budget runner must
record its debit before calling the CLI; this adapter never changes that budget.
Child stdout is data, never authority for charges or completion metadata.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import selectors
import signal
import subprocess
import time

from moriarty_dev import records

FOREMAN_LAUNCHER = Path("/home/charl/foreman/skills/foreman/runtime/dist/foreman-launch.js")


def _contained(root, relative):
    missing = []
    path = records._contained_file(root, relative, missing, "runner")
    if path is None:
        raise ValueError("runner path unavailable: " + ", ".join(missing))
    return path


def _json(path):
    missing = []
    result = records._load_json_path(path, missing, "runner")
    if type(result) is not dict or missing:
        raise ValueError("runner record unavailable: " + ", ".join(missing))
    return result


def _hash(path):
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()


def _check_files(root, mapping, executable):
    if type(mapping) is not dict or executable not in mapping:
        raise ValueError("runner executable is not hash-bound")
    for name, expected in mapping.items():
        if type(name) is not str or type(expected) is not str or len(expected) != 64:
            raise ValueError("invalid runner file commitment")
        path = Path(name)
        if path.is_absolute():
            # External inputs are limited to the interpreter and Foreman itself.
            if name != executable and path != FOREMAN_LAUNCHER:
                raise ValueError("unapproved external runner input")
        else:
            path = _contained(root, name)
        if not path.is_file() or _hash(path) != expected:
            raise ValueError("runner file changed: " + name)


def load_runner(root, action):
    """Resolve through the same campaign and hashed binding as source admission."""
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    program = _json(_contained(root, "openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json"))
    store = program["reportReconciliation"]["stageAdmission"]["campaignRecordStore"]
    campaigns = _json(_contained(root, store))["campaigns"]
    if not action["admissionRef"].startswith("campaign:"):
        raise ValueError("runner requires existing campaign authority")
    campaign_id = action["admissionRef"][len("campaign:"):]
    campaign = campaigns[campaign_id]
    missing = []
    valid, binding = records._verify_binding(root, campaign_id, campaign, missing)
    if not valid or not records._verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, campaign, missing):
        raise ValueError("runner admission changed: " + ", ".join(missing))
    plan = binding.get("runners", {}).get(action["id"])
    if type(plan) is not dict:
        raise ValueError("no authenticated admitted-runner authority is registered")
    required = {"schema", "action", "argv", "files", "launcher", "launcherFiles",
                "timeoutSeconds", "graceSeconds", "outputLimitBytes", "chargeId", "chargedSeconds", "accountingPath"}
    if set(plan) - (required | {"assertion"}) or not required.issubset(plan):
        raise ValueError("unsupported runner binding fields")
    if plan["schema"] != "moriarty.bound-runner/1" or plan["action"] != action or campaign["candidateHash"] != action["candidate"]:
        raise ValueError("runner action/candidate binding mismatch")
    ref, fragment = action["commandRef"].split("#", 1)
    entry = _json(_contained(root, ref))["commands"][fragment]
    argv = plan["argv"]
    if type(argv) is not list or not argv or any(type(x) is not str or not x for x in argv):
        raise ValueError("invalid bound argv")
    if entry != {"argv": argv}:
        raise ValueError("command mapping differs from admitted runner")
    if not Path(argv[0]).is_absolute() or not os.access(argv[0], os.X_OK):
        raise ValueError("runner executable must be an absolute executable path")
    _check_files(root, plan["files"], argv[0])
    for arg in argv[1:]:
        if not arg.startswith("-") and (root / arg).is_file() and arg not in plan["files"]:
            raise ValueError("argv file is not hash-bound: " + arg)
    launcher = plan["launcher"]
    if type(launcher) is not list or len(launcher) != 2 or launcher[1] != str(FOREMAN_LAUNCHER):
        raise ValueError("runner must use the existing Foreman launcher")
    if not Path(launcher[0]).is_absolute() or not os.access(launcher[0], os.X_OK):
        raise ValueError("launcher executable must be absolute")
    if str(FOREMAN_LAUNCHER) not in plan["launcherFiles"]:
        raise ValueError("Foreman bytes are not hash-bound")
    _check_files(root, plan["launcherFiles"], launcher[0])
    for key, low, high in (("timeoutSeconds", 1, 1800), ("graceSeconds", 1, 10), ("outputLimitBytes", 256, 1048576)):
        if type(plan[key]) is not int or not low <= plan[key] <= high:
            raise ValueError("invalid runner bound: " + key)
    if type(plan["chargedSeconds"]) is not int or plan["chargedSeconds"] < plan["timeoutSeconds"] + plan["graceSeconds"] + 30:
        raise ValueError("charge does not cover startup, execution and cleanup")
    if type(plan["chargeId"]) is not str or not plan["chargeId"].strip():
        raise ValueError("runner charge identity unavailable")
    assertion = plan.get("assertion")
    if action["kind"] == "reproduce":
        if type(assertion) is not dict or set(assertion) != {"id", "exitCode", "stdoutSha256"}:
            raise ValueError("reproducer requires an admitted behavioral assertion")
        if type(assertion["id"]) is not str or not assertion["id"].strip():
            raise ValueError("empty behavioral assertion ID")
        if type(assertion["exitCode"]) is not int or not 2 <= assertion["exitCode"] <= 123:
            raise ValueError("behavioral exit must differ from ordinary failure and timeout")
        if type(assertion["stdoutSha256"]) is not str or len(assertion["stdoutSha256"]) != 64:
            raise ValueError("behavioral output commitment unavailable")
    elif assertion is not None:
        raise ValueError("assertion supplied for a non-reproducer")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(plan, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
    if campaign["currentAccounting"]["path"] != plan["accountingPath"]:
        raise ValueError("charge source differs from current campaign accounting")
    budget = _json(_contained(root, plan["accountingPath"]))
    charges = budget["charges"] + budget["externalPackageCharges"]
    matching = [row for row in charges if row.get("id") == plan["chargeId"]]
    if len(matching) != 1:
        raise ValueError("exactly one existing debit is required")
    charge = matching[0]
    expected = {"seconds": plan["chargedSeconds"], "candidateHash": action["candidate"],
                "actionId": action["id"], "runnerDigest": digest}
    if any(charge.get(k) != v for k, v in expected.items()):
        raise ValueError("existing debit is not bound to this runner/action/candidate")
    return plan, digest


def execute(root, plan, digest, reservation_id):
    """Observe the actual process; retain bounded output while hashing all bytes."""
    command = [*plan["launcher"], "--timeout", str(plan["timeoutSeconds"]),
               "--grace", str(plan["graceSeconds"]), "--require-containment", "strong", "--", *plan["argv"]]
    started = time.monotonic()
    proc = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True)
    selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
    streams = {"stdout": bytearray(), "stderr": bytearray()}
    hashes = {name: hashlib.sha256() for name in streams}
    sizes = {name: 0 for name in streams}
    for pipe, name in ((proc.stdout, "stdout"), (proc.stderr, "stderr")):
        selector.register(pipe, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
    limit = plan["outputLimitBytes"]
    ambiguous = False
    try:
        while selector.get_map() or proc.poll() is None:
            if time.monotonic() - started > plan["timeoutSeconds"] + plan["graceSeconds"] + 30:
                ambiguous = True
                os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                break
            for key, _ in selector.select(0.1):
                chunk = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 16384)
                if not chunk:
                    selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                    continue
                name = key.data
                hashes[name].update(chunk)
                sizes[name] += len(chunk)
                streams[name].extend(chunk[:max(0, limit - len(streams[name]))])
        ret = proc.wait(timeout=5)
        # A signal, launcher failure, or namespace signal encoding does not
        # establish completion. Foreman's confirmed timeout uses exit 124.
        ambiguous = ambiguous or ret < 0 or ret >= 125
    finally:
        selector.close()
        proc.stdout.close()
        proc.stderr.close()
    overflow = any(size > limit for size in sizes.values())
    assertion = plan.get("assertion")
    verified = bool(not ambiguous and not overflow and assertion and ret == assertion["exitCode"]
                    and hashes["stdout"].hexdigest() == assertion["stdoutSha256"])
    observed = {"actionId": plan["action"]["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
                "runnerDigest": digest, "chargeId": plan["chargeId"], "reservationId": reservation_id,
                "argv": command, "exitCode": ret, "elapsedSeconds": time.monotonic() - started,
                "outputSha256": {name: h.hexdigest() for name, h in hashes.items()}, "outputBytes": sizes,
                "completionAmbiguous": ambiguous, "outputLimitExceeded": overflow,
                "behavioralAssertions": [{"assertionId": assertion["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
                                          "outcome": "defect-observed"}] if verified else []}
    return {"exitCode": ret, "stdout": streams["stdout"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
            "stderr": streams["stderr"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "runnerReceipt": observed}


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/store.py
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import time
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

SCHEMA_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    requirement TEXT NOT NULL,
    capability TEXT NOT NULL,
    candidate TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reservations (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    requirement TEXT NOT NULL,
    capability TEXT NOT NULL,
    candidate TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    pid INTEGER,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    reserved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    finished_at TEXT,
    receipt_json TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admin_intervals (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    start_time REAL NOT NULL,
    end_time REAL NOT NULL,
    activity_type TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    tx_id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    enqueued_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    delivered_at TEXT,
    details_json TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_lineage
    ON events (repository, requirement, capability);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reservations_status
    ON reservations (repository, status, action_kind);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runner_claims (
    charge_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    reservation_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
);
"""

FORBIDDEN_TX_KEYS = {
    "seed", "sk", "secret", "private", "witness", "private_key",
    "spending_key", "viewing_key", "auth_token", "password"
}


class StoreError(Exception):
    pass


class ReservationConflictError(StoreError):
    pass


LIVE_EVIDENCE_PROFILE = "finalized-financial-settlement"
LEGACY_PRIMARY_KINDS = frozenset(("implement", "repair"))


def get_db_path(repo_root: str | Path) -> Path:
    repo_path = Path(repo_root).resolve()
    git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
    try:
        if git_entry.is_file():
            text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
                gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
                if not gitdir.is_absolute():
                    gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
                commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
                if commondir_file.is_file():
                    cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                    common = Path(cd_text)
                    if not common.is_absolute():
                        common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
                    target = common / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
                    return target
                return gitdir / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
        elif git_entry.is_dir():
            return git_entry / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
    except Exception:
        pass
    fallback = repo_path / ".moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
    return fallback


def _canonical_repo_ids(repo: str | Path) -> list[str]:
    repo_path = Path(repo).resolve()
    ids = [str(repo), str(repo_path)]
    git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
    try:
        if git_entry.is_file():
            text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
                gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
                if not gitdir.is_absolute():
                    gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
                ids.append(str(gitdir))
                commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
                if commondir_file.is_file():
                    cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                    common = Path(cd_text)
                    if not common.is_absolute():
                        common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
                    ids.append(str(common))
        elif git_entry.is_dir():
            ids.append(str(git_entry.resolve()))
    except Exception:
        pass
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for i in ids:
        if i not in seen:
            seen.add(i)
            result.append(i)
    return result


def init_db(db_path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    if db_path.is_file():
        # Safeguard: verify existing file is a valid SQLite DB before executing statements.
        # If corrupt, raise StoreError and preserve original corrupt bytes for diagnosis.
        if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
            try:
                with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
                    header = f.read(16)
                if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
                    raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; invalid file header, preserving original bytes for diagnosis")
                with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=5.0)) as conn_test:
                    conn_test.execute("PRAGMA schema_version;")
            except StoreError:
                raise
            except Exception as exc:
                raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; preserving original bytes for diagnosis: {exc}")

    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=10.0, isolation_level=None)
    try:
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;")
        conn.executescript(SCHEMA_SQL)
    except Exception:
        conn.close()
        raise
    return conn


def _now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def make_history_reader(db_path: Path, *, timeout: float = 10.0):
    def history_reader(lineage: dict) -> dict | None:
