        repo = lineage.get("repository", "")
        req = lineage.get("requirement", "")
        cap = lineage.get("capability", "")
        return get_history(db_path, repo, req, cap, timeout=timeout)
    return history_reader


def _action_start_profile(conn, repo, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id):
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT payload_json FROM events
            WHERE event_kind = 'action_start'
              AND repository = ?
              AND requirement = ?
              AND capability = ?
              AND candidate = ?
              AND action_id = ?
            ORDER BY id DESC
            """,
            (repo, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id),
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall()
        if not rows:
            return None
        profiles = []
        for (raw,) in rows:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else (raw or {})
            except Exception:
                return None
            action = payload.get("action") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
            if not isinstance(action, dict) or "evidenceProfile" not in action:
                return None
            profiles.append(action.get("evidenceProfile"))
        if any(item == LIVE_EVIDENCE_PROFILE for item in profiles):
            return LIVE_EVIDENCE_PROFILE
        distinct = set(profiles)
        if len(distinct) == 1:
            return profiles[0]
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _reservation_occupies_primary(conn, action_kind, repo, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id):
    if action_kind in LEGACY_PRIMARY_KINDS:
        return True
    profile = _action_start_profile(
        conn, repo, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id
    )
    if profile is None:
        return True
    return profile == LIVE_EVIDENCE_PROFILE


def _any_other_primary(conn, exclude_id=None):
    rows = conn.execute(
        """
        SELECT id, repository, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id, action_kind
        FROM reservations WHERE status = 'active'
        """
    ).fetchall()
    for rid, repo, req, cap, cand, act_id, kind in rows:
        if exclude_id is not None and rid == exclude_id:
            continue
        if _reservation_occupies_primary(conn, kind, repo, req, cap, cand, act_id):
            return True
    return False


def get_history(db_path: Path, repo: str, requirement: str, capability: str, *, timeout: float = 10.0) -> dict | None:
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return None

    try:
        if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
            with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
                header = f.read(16)
            if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
                return None
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
    except Exception:
        return None

    try:
        cur = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='events'")
        if not cur.fetchone():
            return None

        repo_ids = _canonical_repo_ids(repo)
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in repo_ids)

        # Total events for repository: schema-only or unrecorded database returns None
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT count(*) FROM events WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
            repo_ids,
        )
        total_events = cur.fetchone()[0]
        if total_events == 0:
            return None

        # This database belongs to one Git common directory. All linked
        # worktrees share its primary slot. A dead parent is ambiguous: its
        # child and charge can survive, so only recorded completion releases it.
        primary_active = _any_other_primary(conn)

        cur = conn.execute(
            f"""
            SELECT event_kind, payload_json FROM events
            WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND requirement = ? AND capability = ?
            ORDER BY id ASC
            """,
            repo_ids + [requirement, capability],
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall()

        unresolved_findings = set()
        reproducer_verified = False
        approach_changed = False

        for kind, payload_raw in rows:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(payload_raw) if isinstance(payload_raw, str) else (payload_raw or {})
            except Exception:
                payload = {}

            if kind == "defect_failure":
                fid = (
                    payload.get("findingId")
                    or payload.get("defectId")
                    or payload.get("id")
                    or payload.get("defectClass")
                )
                if fid:
                    unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
                else:
                    unresolved_findings.add(f"defect-{len(unresolved_findings)+1}")
                reproducer_verified = False
                approach_changed = False

            elif kind == "result_review_failure":
                review_findings = payload.get("findings") or payload.get("blockers") or []
                if not review_findings:
                    fid = payload.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{payload.get('candidateHash', '')[:8]}"
                    unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
                    reproducer_verified = False
                    approach_changed = False

            elif kind == "approach_changed":
                approach_changed = True

            elif kind == "reproducer_verified":
                reproducer_verified = True

            elif kind == "result_review":
                verdict = payload.get("verdict") or payload.get("scopedVerdict")
                if verdict == "APPROVED":
                    resolved = payload.get("resolvedFindings") or payload.get("resolved")
                    if resolved and isinstance(resolved, list):
                        for rf in resolved:
                            unresolved_findings.discard(str(rf))
            # Notice: kind == "defect_resolved" is ignored; author events cannot erase blockers

        # Admin cycles
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"""
            SELECT count(*) FROM events
            WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND event_kind = 'admin_cycle'
            """,
            repo_ids,
        )
        admin_cycle_count = cur.fetchone()[0]

        # Admin intervals
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT count(*) FROM admin_intervals WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
            repo_ids,
        )
        total_intervals = cur.fetchone()[0]

        if total_intervals == 0:
            admin_seconds = None
            admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count if admin_cycle_count > 0 else None
        else:
            cur = conn.execute(
                f"""
                SELECT start_time, end_time FROM admin_intervals
                WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})
                  AND activity_type NOT IN ('test', 'testing', 'afk', 'idle')
                ORDER BY start_time ASC
                """,
                repo_ids,
            )
            intervals = cur.fetchall()
            admin_seconds = int(_merge_intervals(intervals))
            admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count

        return {
            "sameDefectFailures": len(unresolved_findings),
            "adminCycles": admin_cycles,
            "adminSeconds": admin_seconds,
            "primaryActive": primary_active,
            "reproducerVerified": reproducer_verified,
            "approachChanged": approach_changed,
        }
    except Exception:
        return None
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


def read_store_identity(db_path: Path, *, timeout: float = 10.0) -> str | None:
    """Read a persisted identity token if present. Never creates schema or rows."""
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return None
    try:
        uri = "file:" + str(Path(db_path).resolve()) + "?mode=ro"
        conn = sqlite3.connect(uri, uri=True, timeout=timeout)
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='store_identity'"
        )
        if not cur.fetchone():
            return None
        rows = conn.execute("SELECT token FROM store_identity LIMIT 2").fetchall()
        if len(rows) != 1:
            return None
        token = rows[0][0]
        if type(token) is not str or token.strip() == "":
            return None
        return token
    except Exception:
        return None
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


def _exact_lineage_recorded(db_path: Path, repo: str, requirement: str, capability: str, *, timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return False
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
    except Exception:
        return False
    try:
        repo_ids = _canonical_repo_ids(repo)
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in repo_ids)
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT count(*) FROM events WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND requirement = ? AND capability = ?",
            repo_ids + [requirement, capability],
        )
        return cur.fetchone()[0] > 0
    except Exception:
        return False
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


def get_live_history(db_path: Path, repo: str, requirement: str, capability: str, *, identity_anchor=None, timeout: float = 10.0) -> dict | None:
    """Live-profile history: available only on a positive in-store identity match.

    Production callers pass no anchor, so history stays unavailable until a
    separately approved identity adoption. Tests may pass an in-process
    ``identity_anchor`` that must equal the persisted token.
    """
    if type(identity_anchor) is not str or identity_anchor.strip() == "":
        return None
    token = read_store_identity(db_path, timeout=timeout)
    if token is None or token != identity_anchor:
        return None
    if not _exact_lineage_recorded(db_path, repo, requirement, capability, timeout=timeout):
        return None
    return get_history(db_path, repo, requirement, capability, timeout=timeout)


def make_guarded_history_reader(db_path: Path, *, live_lineage=None, identity_anchor=None, timeout: float = 10.0):
    def history_reader(lineage: dict) -> dict | None:
        repo = lineage.get("repository", "")
        req = lineage.get("requirement", "")
        cap = lineage.get("capability", "")
        is_live = False
        if live_lineage is not None:
            try:
                is_live = bool(live_lineage(lineage))
            except Exception:
                is_live = True
        if is_live:
            return get_live_history(
                db_path, repo, req, cap, identity_anchor=identity_anchor, timeout=timeout
            )
        return get_history(db_path, repo, req, cap, timeout=timeout)
    return history_reader


def _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    findings = []
    postmortems = repo_path / "docs" / "postmortems"
    obs_file = postmortems / "2026-09-08-ledger-source-observation.json"
    if obs_file.is_file():
        cand = "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5"
        try:
            obs_data = json.loads(obs_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            cand = obs_data.get("candidateBase", cand)
        except Exception:
            pass
        findings.append({
            "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-01",
            "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
            "requirement": "SP05.1",
            "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
            "candidate": cand,
            "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-06-moriarty-verification-detour.md",
            "description": "September 6 post-mortem recurrence 1: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
        })
        findings.append({
            "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-02",
            "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
            "requirement": "SP05.1",
            "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
            "candidate": cand,
            "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-08-orchestration-recurrence.md",
            "description": "September 8 post-mortem recurrence 2: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
        })
    return findings


def bootstrap_store(db_path: Path, repo: str, initial_findings: list[dict] | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    repo_path = Path(repo).resolve()
    if initial_findings is None:
        initial_findings = _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'bootstrap', 'bootstrap', ?, ?)
                """,
                (str(repo), now, json.dumps({
                    "bootstrappedAt": now,
                    "repository": str(repo),
                    "coverage": [f.get("findingId") for f in initial_findings],
                })),
            )
            for f in initial_findings:
                req = f.get("requirement", "SP05.1")
                cap = f.get("capability", "fixed-financial-driver")
                cand = f.get("candidate", "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5")
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO events (
                        repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                        action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'bootstrap-finding', 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (str(repo), req, cap, cand, now, json.dumps(f)),
                )
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return conn


def _is_pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
        return True
    except (OSError, ProcessLookupError):
        return False


def _merge_intervals(intervals: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> float:
    if not intervals:
        return 0.0
    valid = []
    for pair in intervals:
        try:
            s, e = float(pair[0]), float(pair[1])
            if e >= s:
                valid.append((s, e))
        except (ValueError, TypeError, IndexError):
            continue
    if not valid:
        return 0.0
    valid.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
    merged = [list(valid[0])]
    for s, e in valid[1:]:
        prev = merged[-1]
        if s <= prev[1]:
            prev[1] = max(prev[1], e)
        else:
            merged.append([s, e])
    return sum(e - s for s, e in merged)


def reserve(db_path: Path, repo: str, action: dict, snapshot: dict, charge_id: str | None = None) -> str:
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        action_kind = action.get("kind", "")
        incoming_occupies = (
            action_kind in LEGACY_PRIMARY_KINDS
            or action.get("evidenceProfile") == LIVE_EVIDENCE_PROFILE
        )
        if incoming_occupies and _any_other_primary(conn):
            raise ReservationConflictError(
                "Unresolved primary reservation already exists"
            )

        res_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        if charge_id is not None:
            try:
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO runner_claims (charge_id, reservation_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (charge_id, res_id))
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
                raise ReservationConflictError("Existing charge already claimed: " + charge_id) from exc
        pid = os.getpid()
        now = _now_iso()
        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO reservations (
                id, repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                action_id, action_kind, pid, status, reserved_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active', ?)
            """,
            (
                res_id,
                repo,
                action.get("requirement", ""),
                action.get("capability", ""),
                action.get("candidate", ""),
                action.get("id", ""),
                action_kind,
                pid,
                now,
            ),
        )

        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO events (
                repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_start', ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                repo,
                action.get("requirement", ""),
                action.get("capability", ""),
                action.get("candidate", ""),
                action.get("id", ""),
                now,
                json.dumps({"action": action, "snapshot": snapshot, "reservationId": res_id}),
            ),
        )
        conn.commit()
        return res_id
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def has_other_primary(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str) -> bool:
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db_path)) as conn:
        return _any_other_primary(conn, exclude_id=reservation_id)


def finish(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str, receipt: dict | None = None, status: str = "finished"):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        now = _now_iso()
        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT repository, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id FROM reservations WHERE id = ?",
            (reservation_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        receipt_str = json.dumps(receipt or {})
        conn.execute(
            """
            UPDATE reservations
            SET status = ?, finished_at = ?, receipt_json = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (status, now, receipt_str, reservation_id),
        )
        if row:
            repo, req, cap, cand, act_id = row
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_end', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, req, cap, cand, act_id, now, receipt_str),
            )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_event(
    db_path: Path,
    repo: str,
    requirement: str,
    capability: str,
    candidate: str,
    action_id: str,
    event_kind: str,
    payload: dict,
):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    requirement,
                    capability,
                    candidate,
                    action_id,
                    event_kind,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps(payload),
                ),
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_review(db_path: Path, repo: str, receipt_data: dict, action: dict | None = None):
    if not isinstance(receipt_data, dict):
        raise StoreError("Receipt must be a JSON object")

    author = receipt_data.get("author")
    reviewer = (
        receipt_data.get("reviewer")
        or receipt_data.get("reviewerObserved")
        or receipt_data.get("reviewerRequested")
    )
    if not reviewer or not isinstance(reviewer, str):
        raise StoreError("Reviewer identity required in review receipt")
    if author and isinstance(author, str):
        a_low = author.lower().strip()
        r_low = reviewer.lower().strip()
        if a_low in r_low or r_low in a_low:
            raise StoreError("Author self-review is forbidden")

    cand = receipt_data.get("candidateHash", receipt_data.get("candidate", ""))
    if not cand or not isinstance(cand, str) or len(cand) < 16:
        raise StoreError("Valid candidateHash required in review receipt")

    scope = receipt_data.get("scope", receipt_data.get("auditScope", ""))
    if not scope or not isinstance(scope, str) or scope.strip() == "" or scope.strip().lower() == "none":
        raise StoreError("Valid non-empty scope required in review receipt")

    if action:
        action_cand = action.get("candidate", cand)
        if cand != action_cand:
            raise StoreError(f"Candidate mismatch: receipt candidate {cand} does not match action candidate {action_cand}")
        req = action.get("requirement", receipt_data.get("requirement", ""))
        cap = action.get("capability", receipt_data.get("capability", ""))
        act_id = action.get("id", "review")
    else:
        req = receipt_data.get("requirement", "")
        cap = receipt_data.get("capability", "")
        act_id = receipt_data.get("actionId", "review")

    verdict = receipt_data.get("verdict", receipt_data.get("scopedVerdict", ""))
    findings = receipt_data.get("findings")
    if findings is None:
        findings = receipt_data.get("blockers", [])

    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    try:
        with conn:
            event_kind = "result_review" if verdict == "APPROVED" else "result_review_failure"
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    req,
                    cap,
                    cand,
                    act_id,
                    event_kind,
                    now,
                    json.dumps(receipt_data),
                ),
            )
            if verdict != "APPROVED":
                if not findings:
                    fid = receipt_data.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{cand[:8]}"
                    findings = [{"findingId": fid, "defectClass": "review_rejected", "description": "Review not approved"}]
                for finding in findings:
                    if isinstance(finding, str):
                        finding = {"findingId": finding, "defectClass": finding}
                    conn.execute(
                        """
                        INSERT INTO events (
                            repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                            action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
                        """,
                        (
                            repo,
                            req,
                            cap,
                            cand,
                            act_id,
                            now,
                            json.dumps(finding),
                        ),
                    )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_admin_interval(db_path: Path, repo: str, start_time: float, end_time: float, activity_type: str):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO admin_intervals (repository, start_time, end_time, activity_type)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, start_time, end_time, activity_type),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'admin', 'admin_cycle', ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps({
                        "startTime": start_time,
                        "endTime": end_time,
                        "duration": end_time - start_time,
                        "type": activity_type,
                    }),
                ),
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def enqueue_tx(db_path: Path, repo: str, tx_id: str, status: str, details: dict):
    if status != "submitted":
        raise StoreError("Only a selected public submitted transaction may enter the outbox")
    if not isinstance(tx_id, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", tx_id) or not isinstance(details, dict):
        raise StoreError("Transaction id and public details are required")
    forbidden_subs = ("seed", "sk", "secret", "private", "witness", "password", "token", "auth", "spending_key", "signing_key")
    def _check_forbidden(d):
        if isinstance(d, dict):
            for k, v in d.items():
                k_low = k.lower()
                for sub in forbidden_subs:
                    if sub in k_low and k_low not in ("publicsink", "public_sink"):
                        raise ValueError(f"Forbidden private field in transaction details: {k}")
                _check_forbidden(v)
        elif isinstance(d, list):
            for item in d:
                _check_forbidden(item)

    _check_forbidden(details)
    # Closed public fields: arbitrary notes/objects can contain witness data
    # even when their keys do not look private. The CLI supplies only network.
    if set(details) - {"network", "blockNumber", "contractAddress"}:
        raise ValueError("Only network, blockNumber and contractAddress are public outbox fields")
    if "network" in details and details["network"] != "preview":
        raise ValueError("Only Preview public observations are supported")
    if "blockNumber" in details and (type(details["blockNumber"]) is not int or details["blockNumber"] < 0):
        raise ValueError("Public blockNumber must be a nonnegative integer")
    if "contractAddress" in details and (not isinstance(details["contractAddress"], str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", details["contractAddress"])):
        raise ValueError("Invalid public contractAddress")

    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            existing = conn.execute("SELECT repository FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
            if existing:
                if get_db_path(existing[0]).resolve() != get_db_path(repo).resolve():
                    raise StoreError("Transaction belongs to another repository")
                conn.commit()
                return
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO outbox (repository, tx_id, status, enqueued_at, details_json)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, tx_id, status, _now_iso(), json.dumps(details)),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_notification', ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "status": status, "details": details}),
                ),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


VALID_TX_STATUSES = {"submitted", "unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"}
TX_TRANSITIONS = {
    "submitted": {"unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"},
    "unknown-finality": {"failed", "confirmed"},
    "failed": set(),
    "confirmed": set(),
    "delivered": set(),
}


def update_tx_status(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, new_status: str, note: str | None = None):
    if new_status not in VALID_TX_STATUSES:
        raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction status: {new_status}")
    if note is not None:
        raise StoreError("Free-form notes are not public transaction fields")
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            cur = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, details_json FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            if not row:
                raise StoreError(f"Transaction not found in outbox: {tx_id}")
            repo, old_status, details_str = row
            if new_status == old_status:
                conn.commit()
                return
            if new_status not in TX_TRANSITIONS.get(old_status, set()):
                raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction transition: {old_status} -> {new_status}")
            try:
                details = json.loads(details_str)
            except Exception:
                details = {}
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE outbox SET status = ?, details_json = ?, delivered_at = NULL, enqueued_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
                (new_status, json.dumps(details), _now_iso(), tx_id),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_status_change', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, _now_iso(), json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "newStatus": new_status, "note": note})),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def get_undelivered_txs(db_path: Path, repo: str | None = None, *, timeout: float = 5.0, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return []
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT tx_id, status, details_json, enqueued_at, repository FROM outbox WHERE delivered_at IS NULL ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
            (-1 if limit is None else limit,),
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall()
        result = []
        for tx_id, status, details_str, enq, owner in rows:
            if repo and get_db_path(owner).resolve() != get_db_path(repo).resolve():
                continue
            try:
                details = json.loads(details_str)
            except Exception:
                details = {}
            result.append({
                "txId": tx_id,
                "status": status,
                "enqueuedAt": enq,
                "details": details,
            })
        return result
    finally:
        conn.close()


def mark_delivered(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, acknowledgement: dict | None = None):
    if not isinstance(acknowledgement, dict) or acknowledgement.get("txId") != tx_id:
        raise StoreError("Actual delivery acknowledgement for this transaction is required")
    delivery_id = acknowledgement.get("deliveryId")
    if not isinstance(delivery_id, str) or not delivery_id:
        raise StoreError("Delivery acknowledgement id is required")
    if acknowledgement.get("source") != "codex-session-message" or not acknowledgement.get("contentSha256"):
        raise StoreError("Host-observed message acknowledgement required")
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
            if not row:
                raise StoreError("Transaction not found in outbox")
            repo, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at = row
            if delivered_at is not None:
                raise StoreError("Transaction is already delivered")
            if acknowledgement.get("status") != status or acknowledgement.get("enqueuedAt") != enqueued_at:
                raise StoreError("Delivery observation is stale for current transaction status")
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE outbox SET delivered_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
                (now, tx_id),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_delivered', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, now, json.dumps({**acknowledgement, "deliveredAt": now})),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/tests/test_live_policy_history.py
"""Isolated live-profile policy and review-history regression tests.

Exercises public ``assess`` / store / CLI APIs against tempdir SQLite only.
Does not write a live git common store, budget.lock, manifests, wallets,
or grant live admission. ``assess`` never grants admission.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import io
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

SCRIPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "scripts"
if str(SCRIPTS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(SCRIPTS_DIR))

from moriarty_dev.cli import catalog_live_lineage, cmd_review, cmd_run, cmd_status
from moriarty_dev.policy import assess
from moriarty_dev.records import load_snapshot
from moriarty_dev.store import (
    ReservationConflictError,
    get_db_path,
    get_history,
    get_live_history,
    has_other_primary,
    init_db,
    make_guarded_history_reader,
    record_event,
    record_review,
    read_store_identity,
    reserve,
    finish,
    SCHEMA_SQL,
)

LIVE_PROFILE = "finalized-financial-settlement"
NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE = "local-runtime"
LIVE_HISTORY_KINDS = (
    "verify",
    "review",
    "reproduce",
    "implement",
    "repair",
    "admin",
    "report",
)
LIVE_PRIMARY_KINDS = LIVE_HISTORY_KINDS
LIVE_RETRY_DENIED_KINDS = (
    "verify",
    "review",
    "reproduce",
    "implement",
    "admin",
    "report",
)
REQ = "SP05"
CAP = "loan-executor"
CANDIDATE_HASH = "a" * 64


def live_action(kind, *, profile=LIVE_PROFILE, **overrides):
    body = {
        "id": "live-loan-" + kind,
        "requirement": REQ,
        "capability": CAP,
        "kind": kind,
        "candidate": "diagnosis-only",
        "admissionRef": "campaign:diagnosis-only",
        "commandRef": "diagnosis-only#no-command",
        "evidenceProfile": profile,
    }
    body.update(overrides)
    return body


def snapshot(**overrides):
    body = {
        "authorityCurrent": True,
        "entryEligible": True,
        "candidateCurrent": True,
        "resourceAdmitted": True,
        "sameDefectFailures": 0,
        "adminCycles": 0,
        "adminSeconds": 0,
        "primaryActive": False,
        "reproducerVerified": False,
        "approachChanged": False,
        "nextActionId": None,
        "missingEvidence": [],
    }
    body.update(overrides)
    return body


def reproducer_snapshot():
    """Exact pure-assess loophole inputs from the retained live-kind reproducer."""
    return snapshot(
        sameDefectFailures=2,
        primaryActive=True,
        reproducerVerified=False,
        approachChanged=False,
        nextActionId=None,
        missingEvidence=["operational-history"],
    )


def assert_allowed(test, result, *, context=""):
    test.assertTrue(result["allow"], context or result)
    test.assertEqual(result["reasonCode"], "ALLOWED", context or result)


def assert_denied(test, result, reason, *, context=""):
    test.assertFalse(result["allow"], context or result)
    test.assertEqual(result["reasonCode"], reason, context or result)


class LiveFinancialAssessGates(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_fresh_live_actions_remain_permitted_when_gates_are_clear(self):
        s = snapshot()
        for kind in (
            "verify",
            "review",
            "reproduce",
            "implement",
            "repair",
            "admin",
            "report",
        ):
            assert_allowed(self, assess(s, live_action(kind)), context=kind)

    def test_live_report_obeys_history_retry_and_primary(self):
        s = reproducer_snapshot()
        result = assess(s, live_action("report"))
        assert_denied(self, result, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED")
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(snapshot(primaryActive=True), live_action("report")), "PRIMARY_ACTIVE"
        )
        assert_denied(
            self,
            assess(snapshot(sameDefectFailures=2), live_action("report")),
            "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY",
        )

    def test_history_gate_denies_each_live_dispatch_kind_independently(self):
        s = snapshot(missingEvidence=["operational-history"])
        for kind in LIVE_HISTORY_KINDS:
            result = assess(s, live_action(kind))
            assert_denied(self, result, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED", context=kind)
            self.assertEqual(result["missingEvidence"], ["operational-history"])

    def test_history_prefix_marker_is_also_a_live_history_gap(self):
        s = snapshot(missingEvidence=["operational-history:unverified"])
        result = assess(s, live_action("verify"))
        assert_denied(self, result, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED")

    def test_primary_gate_denies_each_live_dispatch_kind_independently(self):
        s = snapshot(primaryActive=True)
        for kind in LIVE_PRIMARY_KINDS:
            assert_denied(
                self,
                assess(s, live_action(kind)),
                "PRIMARY_ACTIVE",
                context=kind,
            )

    def test_retry_gate_denies_live_nonrepair_kinds_like_implement(self):
        s = snapshot(sameDefectFailures=2, nextActionId="live-loan-reproduce")
        for kind in LIVE_RETRY_DENIED_KINDS:
            result = assess(s, live_action(kind))
            assert_denied(self, result, "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY", context=kind)
            self.assertEqual(result["nextActionId"], "live-loan-reproduce")

    def test_live_reproduce_denied_at_two_failures_even_when_ready(self):
        s = snapshot(
            sameDefectFailures=2,
            reproducerVerified=True,
            approachChanged=True,
        )
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("reproduce")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY"
        )
        assert_denied(self, assess(s, live_action("verify")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY")
        assert_denied(self, assess(s, live_action("report")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY")
        assert_allowed(self, assess(s, live_action("repair")))

    def test_live_repair_still_requires_reproducer_and_changed_approach(self):
        s = snapshot(sameDefectFailures=2)
        assert_denied(self, assess(s, live_action("repair")), "REPAIR_UNREADY")
        s["reproducerVerified"] = True
        s["approachChanged"] = True
        assert_allowed(self, assess(s, live_action("repair")))
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("implement")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY"
        )
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY"
        )
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("reproduce")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY"
        )

    def test_combined_reproducer_snapshot_denies_live_kinds_like_implement(self):
        s = reproducer_snapshot()
        implement = assess(s, live_action("implement"))
        assert_denied(self, implement, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED")
        for kind in ("verify", "review", "reproduce", "report"):
            result = assess(s, live_action(kind))
            assert_denied(self, result, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED", context=kind)

    def test_profile_discriminates_history_exemption(self):
        s = snapshot(missingEvidence=["operational-history"])
        assert_allowed(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE))
        )
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify")), "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED"
        )

    def test_profile_discriminates_primary_exemption(self):
        s = snapshot(primaryActive=True)
        assert_allowed(
            self, assess(s, live_action("review", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE))
        )
        assert_denied(self, assess(s, live_action("review")), "PRIMARY_ACTIVE")

    def test_profile_discriminates_retry_exemption_for_verify(self):
        s = snapshot(sameDefectFailures=2)
        assert_allowed(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE))
        )
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify")), "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY"
        )

    def test_live_verify_still_fail_closed_on_earlier_gates(self):
        resource = snapshot(
            resourceAdmitted=False, missingEvidence=["operational-history"]
        )
        assert_denied(
            self,
            assess(resource, live_action("verify")),
            "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED",
        )
        admission = snapshot(
            missingEvidence=["admissionRef-unresolved:campaign:missing"]
        )
        assert_denied(
            self,
            assess(admission, live_action("reproduce")),
            "ADMISSION_UNRESOLVED",
        )
        stale = snapshot(candidateCurrent=False, primaryActive=True)
        assert_denied(
            self, assess(stale, live_action("review")), "EVIDENCE_STALE"
        )


class NonfinancialAssessControls(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_nonfinancial_verify_review_reproduce_keep_historical_exemptions(self):
        blocked = snapshot(
            sameDefectFailures=2,
            primaryActive=True,
            missingEvidence=["operational-history"],
        )
        for kind in ("verify", "review", "reproduce"):
            assert_allowed(
                self,
                assess(
                    blocked, live_action(kind, profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE)
                ),
                context=kind,
            )
        assert_allowed(
            self,
            assess(blocked, live_action("report", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE)),
        )

    def test_nonfinancial_reproduce_keeps_retry_exemption(self):
        s = snapshot(sameDefectFailures=2)
        assert_allowed(
            self, assess(s, live_action("reproduce", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE))
        )

    def test_nonfinancial_implement_still_hits_history_first(self):
        s = snapshot(missingEvidence=["operational-history"])
        assert_denied(
            self,
            assess(s, live_action("implement", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE)),
            "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED",
        )

    def test_syntax_profile_verify_keeps_nonfinancial_history_exemption(self):
        s = snapshot(missingEvidence=["operational-history"], primaryActive=True)
        assert_allowed(
            self, assess(s, live_action("verify", profile="syntax"))
        )


class IsolatedTempStore(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name) / "isolated-repo"
        self.root.mkdir()
        # No git metadata: get_db_path must use the in-repo fallback, never a
        # live worktree common directory.
        self.db_path = get_db_path(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.db_path, self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
        )
        self.assertFalse((self.root / ".git").exists())
        init_db(self.db_path).close()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def history(self):
        return get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), REQ, CAP)

    def seed_findings(self, *finding_ids):
        for index, finding_id in enumerate(finding_ids):
            record_event(
                self.db_path,
                str(self.root),
                REQ,
                CAP,
                CANDIDATE_HASH,
                "seed-" + str(index),
                "defect_failure",
                {
                    "findingId": finding_id,
                    "defectClass": "retained-loan-executor-defect",
                },
            )

    def approved(self, scope, resolved=None):
        receipt = {
            "author": "grok-4.6",
            "reviewer": "claude-opus-5",
            "candidateHash": CANDIDATE_HASH,
            "scope": scope,
            "verdict": "APPROVED",
            "requirement": REQ,
            "capability": CAP,
        }
        if resolved is not None:
            receipt["resolvedFindings"] = list(resolved)
        return receipt

    def reserve_kind(self, kind, *, profile=LIVE_PROFILE, action_id=None):
        action = live_action(kind, profile=profile)
        if action_id is not None:
            action["id"] = action_id
        return reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snapshot())


class LivePrimaryReservation(IsolatedTempStore):
    def test_store_path_is_isolated_and_creates_no_budget_lock_or_claims(self):
        self.assertTrue(str(self.db_path).startswith(str(self.root)))
        self.assertFalse(any(self.root.rglob("budget.lock")))
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        try:
            claims = conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM runner_claims").fetchone()[0]
        finally:
            conn.close()
        self.assertEqual(claims, 0)

    def test_active_live_verify_occupies_primary_for_readers(self):
        res_id = self.reserve_kind("verify")
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
        self.assertTrue(has_other_primary(self.db_path, "not-" + res_id))
        self.assertFalse(has_other_primary(self.db_path, res_id))
        finish(self.db_path, res_id, {"exitCode": 0})
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertFalse(history["primaryActive"])

    def test_live_verify_conflicts_with_active_implement(self):
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind("implement")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("verify")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_implement_conflicts_with_active_live_verify(self):
        verify_id = self.reserve_kind("verify")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("implement")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, verify_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_live_review_conflicts_with_active_implement(self):
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind("implement", action_id="live-loan-implement-review")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("review")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_live_reproduce_conflicts_with_active_implement(self):
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind(
            "implement", action_id="live-loan-implement-reproduce"
        )
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("reproduce")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_live_report_conflicts_with_active_implement(self):
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind("implement", action_id="live-loan-implement-report")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("report")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_implement_conflicts_with_active_live_report(self):
        report_id = self.reserve_kind("report")
        try:
            history = self.history()
            self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
            self.assertTrue(has_other_primary(self.db_path, "not-" + report_id))
            self.assertFalse(has_other_primary(self.db_path, report_id))
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("implement")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, report_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_second_live_verify_conflicts_like_primary(self):
        first = self.reserve_kind("verify")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("verify", action_id="live-loan-verify-other")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, first, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_live_verify_conflicts_with_active_repair(self):
        repair_id = self.reserve_kind("repair")
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                self.reserve_kind("verify")
        finally:
            finish(self.db_path, repair_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_nonfinancial_verify_does_not_occupy_or_conflict_with_primary(self):
        verify_id = self.reserve_kind("verify", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE)
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertFalse(history["primaryActive"])
        self.assertFalse(has_other_primary(self.db_path, "unrelated"))
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind(
            "implement",
            profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE,
            action_id="local-implement",
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
        finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})
        finish(self.db_path, verify_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_nonfinancial_report_reservation_does_not_occupy_primary(self):
        report_id = self.reserve_kind("report", profile=NONFINANCIAL_PROFILE)
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertFalse(history["primaryActive"])
        impl_id = self.reserve_kind("implement")
        finish(self.db_path, impl_id, {"exitCode": 0})
        finish(self.db_path, report_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_reserve_without_charge_id_does_not_write_runner_claims(self):
        res_id = self.reserve_kind("verify")
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        try:
            claims = conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM runner_claims").fetchone()[0]
        finally:
            conn.close()
        self.assertEqual(claims, 0)
        finish(self.db_path, res_id, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_unknown_active_profile_occupies_primary(self):
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        now = "2026-09-11T00:00:00+00:00"
        try:
            with conn:
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO reservations (
                        id, repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                        action_id, action_kind, pid, status, reserved_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'verify', 1, 'active', ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        "unknown-res",
                        str(self.root),
                        REQ,
                        CAP,
                        CANDIDATE_HASH,
                        "unknown-verify",
                        now,
                    ),
                )
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO events (
                        repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                        action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_start', ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        str(self.root),
                        REQ,
                        CAP,
                        CANDIDATE_HASH,
                        "unknown-verify",
                        now,
                        "{}",
                    ),
                )
        finally:
            conn.close()
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
        self.assertTrue(has_other_primary(self.db_path, "other"))

    def test_concurrent_live_verify_only_one_primary(self):
        winners = []
        conflicts = []

        def worker(action_id):
            try:
                winners.append(self.reserve_kind("verify", action_id=action_id))
            except ReservationConflictError:
                conflicts.append(action_id)

        t1 = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=("live-loan-verify-a",))
        t2 = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=("live-loan-verify-b",))
        t1.start()
        t2.start()
        t1.join()
        t2.join()
        self.assertEqual(len(winners), 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(conflicts), 1)
        self.assertTrue(self.history()["primaryActive"])
        self.assertFalse(has_other_primary(self.db_path, winners[0]))
        finish(self.db_path, winners[0], {"exitCode": 0})


class ScopedReviewHistory(IsolatedTempStore):
    def test_source_specification_resource_approvals_leave_finding_ids(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A", "LIVE-FINDING-B")
        self.assertEqual(self.history()["sameDefectFailures"], 2)
        for scope in ("source", "specification", "resource"):
            record_review(self.db_path, str(self.root), self.approved(scope))
            history = self.history()
            self.assertIsNotNone(history)
            self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 2, scope)

    def test_design_approval_without_named_ids_leaves_findings(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A", "LIVE-FINDING-B")
        record_review(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            self.approved("RP01-MC02 specified design consistency only"),
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 2)

    def test_other_lineage_named_resolution_does_not_clear_this_lineage(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A")
        receipt = self.approved("actual-result", resolved=["LIVE-FINDING-A"])
        receipt["requirement"] = "SP01.6"
        receipt["capability"] = "loan-swap-subset"
        record_review(self.db_path, str(self.root), receipt)
        history = self.history()
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)

    def test_actual_result_explicit_resolution_removes_only_named_findings(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A", "LIVE-FINDING-B")
        record_review(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            self.approved("actual-result", resolved=["LIVE-FINDING-A"]),
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)
        record_review(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            self.approved("actual-result", resolved=["LIVE-FINDING-B"]),
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 0)

    def test_explicit_resolution_does_not_invent_missing_ids(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A", "LIVE-FINDING-B")
        record_review(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            self.approved("actual-result", resolved=["LIVE-FINDING-MISSING"]),
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 2)

    def test_author_defect_resolved_event_cannot_erase_findings(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A")
        record_event(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            REQ,
            CAP,
            CANDIDATE_HASH,
            "author-claim",
            "defect_resolved",
            {"findingId": "LIVE-FINDING-A"},
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertIsNotNone(history)
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)

    def test_lineage_keeps_findings_after_candidate_rename_events(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-STABLE")
        record_event(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            REQ,
            CAP,
            "b" * 64,
            "renamed-packet",
            "defect_failure",
            {
                "findingId": "LIVE-FINDING-STABLE",
                "defectClass": "retained-loan-executor-defect",
            },
        )
        history = self.history()
        self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)


class IsolationControls(IsolatedTempStore):
    def test_no_git_dir_and_no_lock_after_policy_and_store_use(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A")
        record_review(
            self.db_path, str(self.root), self.approved("source")
        )
        res_id = self.reserve_kind("report")
        finish(self.db_path, res_id, {"exitCode": 0})
        self.assertFalse((self.root / ".git").exists())
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "budget.lock").exists())
        self.assertFalse(any(self.root.rglob("budget.lock")))
        self.assertEqual(
            self.db_path.resolve().parent, (self.root / ".moriarty-dev").resolve()
        )
        self.assertNotIn("store_identity", SCHEMA_SQL)


class CanonicalHistoryAndCli(IsolatedTempStore):
    def write_live_action(self, **overrides):
        action = live_action("verify", **overrides)
        action["candidate"] = CANDIDATE_HASH
        (self.root / ".moriarty-dev").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "actions.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"schema": "moriarty-dev.actions/1", "actions": [action]})
        )
        return action

    def guarded_reader(self, **kwargs):
        return make_guarded_history_reader(
            self.db_path,
            live_lineage=catalog_live_lineage(self.root),
            **kwargs,
        )

    def write_fixture_identity(self, token):
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        try:
            with conn:
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE store_identity (token TEXT NOT NULL)")
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO store_identity (token) VALUES (?)", (token,)
                )
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def test_schema_sql_does_not_create_identity(self):
        self.assertNotIn("store_identity", SCHEMA_SQL)
        self.assertIsNone(read_store_identity(self.db_path))

    def test_missing_identity_yields_unavailable_live_history(self):
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A")
        self.assertIsNone(
            get_live_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), REQ, CAP)
        )
        self.assertIsNone(
            get_live_history(
                self.db_path, str(self.root), REQ, CAP, identity_anchor="fixture-anchor-1"
            )
        )

    def test_unrelated_events_do_not_create_verified_zero_live_history(self):
        record_event(
            self.db_path, str(self.root), "", "", "", "seed", "admin_cycle", {}
        )
        self.write_fixture_identity("fixture-anchor-1")
        self.assertIsNone(
            get_live_history(
                self.db_path, str(self.root), REQ, CAP, identity_anchor="fixture-anchor-1"
            )
        )

    def test_anchored_fixture_counts_exact_lineage_through_actual_reader(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A", "LIVE-FINDING-B")
        self.write_fixture_identity("fixture-anchor-1")
        hist = get_live_history(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            REQ,
            CAP,
            identity_anchor="fixture-anchor-1",
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(hist)
        self.assertEqual(hist["sameDefectFailures"], 2)
        reader = self.guarded_reader(identity_anchor="fixture-anchor-1")
        snap = load_snapshot(str(self.root), "live-loan-verify", history_reader=reader)
        self.assertEqual(snap["sameDefectFailures"], 2)
        self.assertNotIn("operational-history", snap["missingEvidence"])

    def test_cli_seeded_unrelated_event_is_unavailable_not_zero(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        record_event(
            self.db_path, str(self.root), "", "", "", "seed", "admin_cycle", {}
        )
        reader = self.guarded_reader()
        snap = load_snapshot(str(self.root), "live-loan-verify", history_reader=reader)
        self.assertIsNone(snap["sameDefectFailures"])
        self.assertIn("operational-history", snap["missingEvidence"])
        buf = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf):
            cmd_status(self.root, self.db_path, reader, True)
        data = json.loads(buf.getvalue())
        self.assertIn("operational-history", data["missingEvidence"])

    def test_cli_renamed_capability_is_unavailable_not_zero(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        self.seed_findings("LIVE-FINDING-A")
        action = live_action("verify")
        action["candidate"] = CANDIDATE_HASH
        action["capability"] = "renamed-loan-executor"
        (self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "actions.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"schema": "moriarty-dev.actions/1", "actions": [action]})
        )
        reader = self.guarded_reader()
        snap = load_snapshot(str(self.root), "live-loan-verify", history_reader=reader)
        self.assertIsNone(snap["sameDefectFailures"])
        self.assertIn("operational-history", snap["missingEvidence"])

    def test_cli_run_does_not_launch_live_action(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        marker = self.root / "child-marker.txt"
        reader = self.guarded_reader()
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as caught:
            cmd_run(self.root, self.db_path, reader, "live-loan-verify", False)
        self.assertIn(caught.exception.code, (2, 3))
        self.assertFalse(marker.exists())
        self.assertTrue(str(self.db_path).startswith(str(self.root)))

    def test_cli_review_authority_rejection_is_preexisting(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        (self.root / "review.json").write_text(json.dumps(self.approved("source")))
        err = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as caught:
                cmd_review(
                    self.root, self.db_path, "review.json", True, "live-loan-verify"
                )
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, 3)
        self.assertIn("authority unavailable", err.getvalue())

    def test_status_does_not_create_identity_table(self):
        self.write_live_action()
        reader = self.guarded_reader()
        buf = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf):
            cmd_status(self.root, self.db_path, reader, True)
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        try:
            names = [
                row[0]
                for row in conn.execute(
                    "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
                ).fetchall()
            ]
        finally:
            conn.close()
        self.assertNotIn("store_identity", names)
        self.assertIsNone(read_store_identity(self.db_path))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/tests/test_execution.py
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

# Ensure scripts and tests directories are importable
SCRIPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "scripts"
TESTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(SCRIPTS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(SCRIPTS_DIR))
if str(TESTS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(TESTS_DIR))

import test_records as tr
from moriarty_dev.store import (
    get_db_path,
    init_db,
    reserve,
    finish,
    record_event,
    record_review,
    record_admin_interval,
    enqueue_tx,
    get_undelivered_txs,
    mark_delivered,
    get_history,
    ReservationConflictError,
    StoreError,
)
from moriarty_dev.policy import assess
from moriarty_dev.records import load_snapshot


class ExecutionTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
        # git init
        subprocess.run(["git", "init"], cwd=str(self.root), capture_output=True, check=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "test@moriarty.local"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Test User"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
        
        self.db_path = get_db_path(self.root)
        init_db(self.db_path).close()
        self.child_marker = self.root / "child-marker.txt"

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def make_case(self, failures=0, action_kind="implement", candidate=None):
        # Set up genuine loan review family in self.root
        tr.copy_genuine_registers(self.root)
        campaigns = tr.load_json(self.root / "evidence" / "moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07" / "report-reconciliation" / "campaign-admission.json")
        tr.write_current_accounting(self.root)
        tr.attach_current_accounting(self.root, campaigns)

        # Write commands.json
        cmd_file = self.root / "commands.json"
        candidate_value = candidate or tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE
        action_id = "sp01-loan-" + action_kind
        runner_program = (
            "import json; from pathlib import Path; "
            f"Path({str(self.child_marker)!r}).write_text('one launch\\n'); "
            f"print(json.dumps({{'candidateHash': {candidate_value!r}, 'actionId': {action_id!r}, "
            "'charged': True, 'chargeId': 'test-admitted-charge-01', "
            f"'behavioralAssertions': [{{'candidateHash': {candidate_value!r}, 'assertionId': 'test-defect', 'outcome': 'defect-observed'}}]}})); "
            "raise SystemExit(1)"
        )
        cmd_data = {
            "schema": "moriarty-dev.commands/1",
            "commands": {
                "driver": {"admittedRunner": {
                    "argv": [
                        sys.executable,
                        "-c",
                        runner_program,
                    ],
                    "actionId": action_id,
                    "candidateHash": candidate_value,
                    "timeoutSeconds": 30,
                }}
            }
        }
        cmd_file.write_text(json.dumps(cmd_data))

        action_def = tr.loan_action(action_kind, "commands.json#driver")
        if candidate:
            action_def["candidate"] = candidate

        tr.write_actions(self.root, [action_def, tr.loan_action("report")])

        for i in range(failures):
            record_event(
                self.db_path,
                str(self.root),
                action_def["requirement"],
                action_def["capability"],
                action_def["candidate"],
                action_def["id"],
                "defect_failure",
                {"defectId": f"DEFECT-{i+1}", "details": "failing test"},
            )

        return action_def

    def run_cli(self, *args):
        cli_py = SCRIPTS_DIR / "moriarty_dev" / "cli.py"
        cmd = [sys.executable, str(cli_py), "--repo", str(self.root)] + list(args)
        return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)

    def test_denied_launch_has_no_child_effect(self):
        self.make_case(failures=2, action_kind="implement")
        res = self.run_cli("run", "--action", "sp01-loan-implement")
        self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 2)
        self.assertFalse(self.child_marker.exists())

    def test_restart_preserves_failure(self):
        self.make_case(failures=2, action_kind="implement", candidate=tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE)
        res1 = self.run_cli("run", "--action", "sp01-loan-implement")
        self.assertEqual(res1.returncode, 2)

        # Rename candidate in action: failure remains because lineage (repo, req, cap) is unchanged
        self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="implement", candidate="other-candidate")
        res2 = self.run_cli("run", "--action", "sp01-loan-implement")
        self.assertEqual(res2.returncode, 2)
        self.assertFalse(self.child_marker.exists())

    def test_untrusted_reproducer_mapping_does_not_launch(self):
        self.make_case(failures=2, action_kind="reproduce")
        res1 = self.run_cli("run", "--action", "sp01-loan-reproduce")
        self.assertEqual(res1.returncode, 2, res1.stderr)
        self.assertIn("no authenticated admitted-runner authority", res1.stderr)
        self.assertFalse(self.child_marker.exists())

        # A mutable command mapping and self-reported charge cannot verify a defect.
        history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertFalse(history["reproducerVerified"])

    def test_concurrent_reservation_denial(self):
        action = self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="implement")
        snap = {"primaryActive": False}
        res_id1 = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
        self.assertTrue(res_id1)

        # Second reservation for primary implementation must fail
        with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
            reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)

        # Finish first reservation
        finish(self.db_path, res_id1, {"exitCode": 0})

        # Now reservation succeeds
        res_id2 = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
        self.assertTrue(res_id2)
        finish(self.db_path, res_id2, {"exitCode": 0})

    def test_linked_worktrees_share_db(self):
        readme = self.root / "README.md"
        readme.write_text("initial")
        subprocess.run(["git", "add", "README.md"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-m", "initial commit"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)

        wt_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="wt-test-"))
        try:
            subprocess.run(["git", "worktree", "add", "-b", "branch-b", str(wt_dir)], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)
            wt_db_path = get_db_path(wt_dir)
            self.assertEqual(wt_db_path.resolve(), self.db_path.resolve())

            action = {"id": "worktree-primary", "kind": "implement"}
            first = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, {})
            with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
                reserve(wt_db_path, str(wt_dir), action, {})
            finish(self.db_path, first, {"exitCode": 0})
            successor = reserve(wt_db_path, str(wt_dir), action, {})
            finish(wt_db_path, successor, {"exitCode": 0})

            record_event(wt_db_path, str(self.root), "SP05", "fixed-financial-driver", "cand-x", "act-x", "defect_failure", {})
            history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP05", "fixed-financial-driver")
            self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)
        finally:
            subprocess.run(["git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", str(wt_dir)], cwd=str(self.root), check=False, capture_output=True)
            shutil.rmtree(wt_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_crashed_reservation_recovery(self):
        action = self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="implement")
        snap = {"primaryActive": False}
        res_id = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)

        # Simulate dead process by setting PID to a non-existent PID (e.g. 999999)
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        with conn:
            conn.execute("UPDATE reservations SET pid = 999999 WHERE id = ?", (res_id,))
        conn.close()

        # A dead parent says nothing about its child or durable charge. Keep
        # this reservation blocking until actual completion is reconciled.
        with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
            reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
        history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), action["requirement"], action["capability"])
        self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        self.assertEqual(conn.execute("SELECT status FROM reservations WHERE id = ?", (res_id,)).fetchone()[0], "active")
        conn.close()

    def test_dead_parent_remains_active_in_read_only_history(self):
        action = self.make_case(action_kind="implement")
        res_id = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, {})
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        conn.execute("UPDATE reservations SET pid = 999999 WHERE id = ?", (res_id,))
        conn.close()
        history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), action["requirement"], action["capability"])
        self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])

    def test_outbox_rejects_private_fields(self):
        # Good public notification
        enqueue_tx(self.db_path, str(self.root), "tx-12345", "submitted", {"blockNumber": 42})
        undelivered = get_undelivered_txs(self.db_path, str(self.root))
        self.assertEqual(len(undelivered), 1)
        self.assertEqual(undelivered[0]["txId"], "tx-12345")

        # Forbidden secret keys
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            enqueue_tx(self.db_path, str(self.root), "tx-secret", "submitted", {"seed": "supersecret"})

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            enqueue_tx(self.db_path, str(self.root), "tx-witness", "submitted", {"witness": "privatedata"})

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            enqueue_tx(self.db_path, str(self.root), "tx-sk", "submitted", {"spending_key": "key"})

    def test_admin_intervals_and_stop_rule(self):
        record_admin_interval(self.db_path, str(self.root), 1000.0, 2800.0, "report_formatting")
        history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP05", "fixed-financial-driver")
        self.assertEqual(history["adminSeconds"], 1800)
        self.assertEqual(history["adminCycles"], 1)

        admin_action = tr.loan_action("admin")
        dec = assess({
            "authorityCurrent": True,
            "entryEligible": True,
            "candidateCurrent": True,
            "resourceAdmitted": True,
            "sameDefectFailures": 0,
            "adminCycles": 1,
            "adminSeconds": 1800,
            "primaryActive": False,
            "reproducerVerified": False,
            "approachChanged": False,
            "nextActionId": None,
            "missingEvidence": [],
        }, admin_action)
        self.assertFalse(dec["allow"])
        self.assertEqual(dec["reasonCode"], "ADMIN_LIMIT")

    def test_public_cli_rejects_unrelated_candidate_review(self):
        self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="review")
        receipt_file = self.root / "receipt-unrelated-candidate.json"
        receipt_file.write_text(json.dumps({
            "requirement": "SP01.6",
            "capability": "loan-swap-subset",
            "candidateHash": "f" * 64,
            "scope": "RP01-MC02 specified design consistency only",
            "author": "grok-4-6",
            "reviewer": "gpt-6-astra",
            "verdict": "APPROVED",
        }))
        res = self.run_cli("review", str(receipt_file))
        self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 3)
        self.assertIn("candidateHash", res.stderr)
        history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertIsNone(history)

    def test_public_cli_rejects_unrelated_scope_review(self):

    def test_public_cli_accepts_genuine_current_non_author_review(self):
        self.make_case(failures=1, action_kind="review")
        hist_before = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertEqual(hist_before["sameDefectFailures"], 1)

        receipt_file = self.root / "receipt-genuine.json"
        receipt_file.write_text(json.dumps({
            "requirement": "SP01.6",
            "capability": "loan-swap-subset",
            "candidateHash": tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE,
            "scope": "RP01-MC02 specified design consistency only",
            "author": "grok-4-6",
            "reviewer": "gpt-6-astra",
            "verdict": "APPROVED",
        }))
        res = self.run_cli("review", str(receipt_file))
        self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 0, f"CLI review failed: {res.stderr}")
        hist_after = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertEqual(hist_after["sameDefectFailures"], 1)

    def test_corrupt_history_and_safe_writes(self):
        self.db_path.write_bytes(b"corrupt sqlite header not valid database")
        hist = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertIsNone(hist)
        with self.assertRaises(StoreError):
            init_db(self.db_path)
        self.assertEqual(self.db_path.read_bytes(), b"corrupt sqlite header not valid database")

    def test_schema_only_history_returns_none(self):
        hist = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertIsNone(hist)

    def test_invalid_row_handled_conservatively(self):
        record_event(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            "SP01.6",
            "loan-swap-subset",
            tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE,
            "act1",
            "defect_failure",
            {"defectId": "D1"},
        )
        conn = init_db(self.db_path)
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset", tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE, "act1", "defect_failure", "2026-09-08T00:00:00Z", "not-a-json{"),
            )
        conn.close()
        hist = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP01.6", "loan-swap-subset")
        self.assertIsNotNone(hist)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(hist["sameDefectFailures"], 1)

    def test_unauthorized_finding_resolution_cannot_erase_blocker(self):

    def test_admin_timing_unknown_and_union_and_exclusion(self):
        record_event(
            self.db_path,
            str(self.root),
            "SP01.6",
            "loan-swap-subset",
            tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE,
            "act1",
            "defect_failure",

        )

        self.assertIsNone(hist["adminSeconds"])

        record_admin_interval(self.db_path, str(self.root), 150.0, 250.0, "review")
        record_admin_interval(self.db_path, str(self.root), 300.0, 400.0, "test")
        record_admin_interval(self.db_path, str(self.root), 400.0, 500.0, "afk")
